---

## Phase ordering: why US2 runs before US1

The story phases below are **not** in bare priority order, and that is deliberate.
plan.md states the sequence is "set by verifiability rather than by user value": the
per-class census (US2, P2) is the acceptance instrument for every other slice --
"a phase is not done when its unit tests pass; it is done when the census run for its
predicate exits 0 with the predicate satisfied"
([contracts/fidelity-census.md](contracts/fidelity-census.md):486-487). US1 cannot be
accepted before the instrument that measures it exists.

Story phase order follows research.md:278 exactly:

```text
Phase 3 (US2 census) -> Phase 4 (US1 natural key) -> Phase 5 (US4 enrich) || Phase 6 (US5a)
                     -> Phase 7 (US3 closure) -> Phase 8 (US5b) -> Phase 9 (Polish)
```

US5 is split across two phases because R5 found a fourth blocking condition: 6 of 18 live
rules reference `PhSimpleContext*` objects owned by `PhPhonData.ContextsOS`, which is a
closure problem. **4a** (wholly-owned graphs) needs only US1; **4b** needs US3.

**037 has no spec artifacts.** There is no `specs/037*` on any branch or worktree, and 037's
`.specify/feature.json` still points at `specs/035-fullsweep-fidelity`. The only coordination
surface with 037 is its branch diff plus [census-evidence.md](census-evidence.md) section 4.
No task below may be phrased as "check 037's tasks.md".

**Two hazards plan.md:280-290 does not list:**

1. `Lib/models.py` (+56) and `Lib/report.py` (+30) are modified by 037 but **claimed by
   nobody**, and this feature names both as primary surfaces (plan.md:174,176). 037 adds
   `LeafExecutionFailure`, `RunReport.leaf_execution_failures` and the `leaf_failed`
   property to exactly those files. They belong on the rebase-first and claim list.
2. **`lockout` claims are worktree-path-scoped.** All seven of 037's live claims name
   `...\GramTrans-037-phon-nc-features\...` paths, so a claim taken from
   `..\GramTrans-038-transfer-fidelity-gaps\` on its own copy of `categories.py` can never
   register as a conflict. Claim the **main-worktree path** and verify with
   `lockout status --file` against 037's absolute path, or the claim is theatre.

**The flexicon floor moved under this plan.** 037 has raised `pyproject.toml` to
`pyflexicon>=4.5.0` (flexicon issue #222 - `NaturalClassOperations` `FeaturesOA` wiring);
plan.md and quickstart.md still say `>=4.4.1`. T002 verifies 4.5.0 and records the
discrepancy rather than editing `pyproject.toml` or `CLAUDE.md`, both of which 037 claims.
A related 037 change is worth knowing here: `natural_classes_execute_action` now **raises**
`RuntimeError` rather than reporting a defective transfer as successful, which is the same
no-silent-skip posture FR-013 and SC-010 require - build on it, do not re-litigate it.

**Textual conflict to expect:** 037's `transfer.py` hunk at `@@ -439` and
`038-affix-fidelity`'s at `@@ -442` are ~3 lines apart, both inside `execute()`. In
`categories.py` the two are cleanly separated (037 is entirely `>= L8009`; 038-affix is
L4851-6483), so that file is only a two-way concern.

**Git split (CLAUDE.md protocol):** everything under `specs/` commits to `main`; all code
commits on the `038-transfer-fidelity-gaps` worktree.

## Phase 3: US2 - The run report tells the truth about what moved (P2, runs first)

**Goal**: a per-class source/destination comparison with a machine-readable gate artifact, so every later slice is accepted by a census diff rather than by a unit test.

**Independent Test**: run the comparison against a completed transfer and confirm the reported per-class figures match the projects. Delivers value alone - a person can see what a transfer lost with no other fix in place.

That is the 5.2 cap behaving as specified, and it is still an unsafe default for a release gate -- the headline says success on a catastrophically incomplete transfer, and the failing evidence surfaces only via `--phase`. Either require `--phase` for a passing gate, or make a gross-basis pass a distinct non-success outcome, so no caller can read exit 0 as "nothing was lost". `evaluate_phase(1)`/`(5)` already fail correctly and name `MoStemMsa` and `MoAffixProcess`, so the information exists; the default is what is wrong - `src/gramtrans/census_cli.py`

It does not: `transfer_run_block` (`census_cli.py:661`) reads only `run_id`, `mode`, `started_at` and `selected_categories` into a provenance block. No per-class tally is read, so the out-of-scope lexicon classes stay in `unexplained_shortfall`.

The meaningful delta is then the rows with `difference != 0` that the run report does NOT account for. Reproducible from a backup, attributable to one named run, and exact. - [x] **T024d** [US2] **DONE 2026-08-19 (worktree `fe16eea`), both halves.** **T024d-a** (`Lib/models.py`, `Lib/report.py`): `RunReport.matched_by_class` (keyed by LCM object class) + `matches_unattributed` (keyed by category) + `matched_to_source_total` / `matched_class_is_complete`, produced in `build_from_plan` and surfaced additively as `matched_to_source` in the snapshot (omit-when-empty, so a run with no matches is byte-identical). Attribution reads ONLY the two authoritative class names -- `MatchBasisRecord.object_class` and `EnrichmentRecord.object_class` -- and routes everything else to the unattributed bucket rather than guessing through the non-1:1 category->class mapping. **T024d-b** (`census_cli.py`): `matched_by_class_from_report` + `_row_for_entry` now earns `baseline_matched` per row when there is a baseline count, a tally for THAT class, and complete attribution; `starter_excluded` then becomes `unmatched_starter`. Verified on 5.2's own worked example: a blank target shipping 23 starter phonemes with the destination at 41 reported `difference` **-23** on the gross basis and reports **0** on the matched basis. Conservative by design -- an absent class is no evidence (never a zero), any unattributed match spoils completeness for every class, and A1 split rows never reach the matched basis. Note the sequencing premise below still holds for T024c, which remains open. ORIGINAL FINDING: Found 2026-08-19 while designing T024c's live rebuild; this is the root cause behind T024b and the reason a "meaningful delta" is not obtainable today. `census.py` supports the basis completely -- it is in `SUBTRACTION_BASES` (`census.py:2022`), `unmatched_starter()` computes from `starter_matched_to_source` (`census.py:2142`), invariant checking special-cases it (`census.py:3018`), the 5.2 cap skips rows carrying it (`gross_basis_suppressions`, `census.py:3240`), and phase evaluation treats its shortfalls as trustworthy (`census.py:3402`). But the only emitter, `_row_for_entry`, hardcodes `basis = "baseline_gross"` on every path that has a baseline, and its own docstring concedes it: *"THE SUBTRACTION BASIS IS NEVER `baseline_matched` HERE ... reading per-class matched tallies out of a run report is T022's seam, not this one's"* (`census_cli.py:688`). **T022 is marked `[x]` and did not do this** -- it attached the census to the run report, not the run report to the census. Consequences:
  1. **Every census on this instrument is advisory, always.** The run verdict is structurally incapable of exceeding `CENSUS_ACCOUNTED`, regardless of baseline kind, run report, or how correct the transfer was. `CENSUS_CLEAN` is unreachable on any real pair.
  2. **The cap's own remediation advice is unfollowable.** `gross_basis_cap_notes` prints *"supply the run report to get a baseline_matched basis and a trustworthy answer"* (`census.py:3297`). Supplying the run report does not produce that basis. The instrument tells the operator to do the one thing that cannot help.
  3. **It mis-reports CORRECT runs, which is the failure mode 5.2 exists to name.** On a target restored blank (`PhPhoneme` starter 23), a transfer that correctly matches all 23 starter phonemes to source phonemes lands the destination at 41. Gross subtraction gives 41 - 23 = 18 and reports `difference` **-23** -- a 23-unit shortfall on a lossless run. Until the matched tally is read, "meaningful delta" cannot be separated from baseline arithmetic.

  **The work:** read per-class identity-substitution / matched counts out of the `RunReport` snapshot in `transfer_run_block`, thread them into `_row_for_entry` as `starter_matched_to_source`, and emit `basis = "baseline_matched"` for rows the report covers. Everything downstream already handles it. **CONFIRMED 2026-08-19: it splits.** `RunReport` does carry substitution tallies -- `CategoryReport.identity_substitution` (`models.py:2651`), summed by `RunReport.identity_substituted` (`models.py:2759`) -- but they are keyed by **`GrammarCategory`**, while every census row is keyed by **LCM object class**. `per_category[PHONEMES].identity_substitution` cannot be attributed to `PhPhoneme` without a category->class mapping, and the mapping is not 1:1 for the affix and MSA categories. So T024d-a is a `report.py` change (tally substitutions per object class, not merely per category) and T024d-b is the `census_cli.py` wiring that consumes it. Do NOT attempt the wiring first: without per-class tallies there is nothing to wire. Sequence T024d BEFORE T024c's live rebuild: running the full-copy simulation without it produces the same capped, uninterpretable exit 0 the rebuild exists to eliminate - `src/gramtrans/census_cli.py`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/report.py`

- [x] **T024e** [US2] **Test the two selection modes -- default PRESELECTION vs force-all -- and pin their difference exactly.** Raised by the user 2026-08-19: with everything selected, some items are deliberately NOT preselected on the assumption they are not needed, and both modes need programmatic testing. **CORRECTED: an earlier draft of this task answered the wrong question** -- it described `_phon_is_empty`, the inventory-level blank drop, and proposed adding a `Selection` switch and a `DroppedItemRecord` channel before the two-mode test could run. That is a different, smaller mechanism (see T024f) and **none of that work is required here.** Both modes are constructible today from existing public functions:
  * **Force-all** is `build_full_selection(exclude=frozenset())`. `collapse_phonology` (`selection.py:2848`) records `leaf_item_picks[cat]` *"ONLY when the category is trimmed ... omitted when all rows are checked (=> transfer-all)"*, so leaving every pick-set empty means transfer-all and the preselection heuristics are bypassed entirely. This is what `debug/run_fullcopy_sweep.py` already runs.
  * **Default/filtered** is what the GUI produces: build the inventory, take `checked = {r.guid for r in group.rows if r.preselected}` per category, and fold it through `collapse_phonology(inventory, checked)` into the Selection. No new API.

  **The two heuristics that leave enumerated items unchecked:**
  1. **Orphan natural classes** (`selection.py:2687`, applied at `:2832` as `replace(r, preselected=(r.guid not in orphan_nc_guids))`). FLEx auto-creates a fresh *"Created automatically for rule X"* NC every time a rule's context is re-saved and strands the previous copy, so a source accumulates dozens nothing references. Orphan-hood is authoritative LCM `ReferringObjects.Count == 0` via `compute_orphan_nc_guids` -- explicitly NOT a re-walk of rule structure, which misses NC contexts nested in `PhSequenceContext`/`StrucChange` and mislabels every used NC an orphan (live-proven on `Mbugwe LizzieHC practice`).
  2. **AS-NEEDED dependency closure** for slots, templates and POS (`selection.py:1174-1197`, `:1510`, `:1560-1586`). A slot is preselected iff a picked affix fills it, a template iff any referenced slot is filled, a POS iff a picked affix attaches to it. With all affixes picked these preselect transitively; anything no pick reaches opens unchecked.

  **Why this is cheap to test:** a non-preselected item is still ENUMERATED -- `preselected` is a field on the row, not a removal -- so the expected difference between the modes is computable from the inventory BEFORE either run, with no run report and no baseline. `orphan_nc_guids` is also an injectable parameter (`None` = compute from source; tests inject a set), so the heuristic can be disabled without touching production defaults. **The assertion:** run both modes against the same restored-blank target and require `after_forceall - after_filtered` to equal EXACTLY the non-preselected GUID set -- larger means the filtered run dropped something the heuristic never claimed, smaller means force-all is not actually forcing. Both directions are real defects and neither is visible in a count-based census - `tests/integration/`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/selection.py`

- [x] **T024i** [US2] **DONE 2026-08-19 (worktree `c8a1d7f`). Out-of-band: the census instrument was CORRUPTING the projects it measured.** Reported by the user as an intermittent "Can't add EN writing system". `flexicon.FLExInitialize()` initialises the SLDR via `Sldr.Initialize(True)`; `flexicon.FLExCleanup()` calls `Sldr.Cleanup()` and takes it down again -- both process-global. Every standalone entry point guarded initialisation with a once-per-process boolean latch, which records that we CALLED FLExInitialize, not whether the SLDR is still up. So within one process: init (SLDR up, latch True) -> any `HostSession.release()`/teardown (SLDR **down**, latch still True) -> the next `OpenProject` short-circuits the latch and opens with the SLDR down. LibLCM's LDML-in-folder repository then fails to parse EVERY file in `WritingSystemStore/`, declares each bad, and RENAMES it to `*.ldml.bad` (`Exception: The SLDR has not been initialized.`); once `en.ldml` is gone the project has no English writing system. **A READ-ONLY open is enough** -- `writeEnabled=False` protects the `.fwdata`, not the writing-system store -- and the census opens BOTH projects read-only, so it was one of the paths doing the damage. Measured fallout in one day: **8 files quarantined in `Esperanto`** (documented read-only in the strong sense) and **2 in `Ejagham Full GT-Test`**, leaving neither project a single valid `.ldml`. Verified live: `after ensure_flex_initialized True / after FLExCleanup False / after re-ensure True`. Fix: new `Lib/flexinit.py` owns the discipline -- FLExInitialize stays once-per-process (registry/ICU) but `Sldr.IsInitialized` is re-checked on EVERY call and re-initialised when down, fail-soft throughout -- wired into `Lib/census.py`, `standalone/app.py` (itself the `FLExCleanup` caller), both `gramtrans.py` opens, and `tests/integration/harness/full_run.py`. The quarantined LDML was restored by rename with the user's explicit authorisation, preserving Esperanto's fr/hbo/id tailoring a FieldWorks regeneration would have replaced with defaults; `Esperanto.fwdata` was not touched. `tests/unit/test_flexinit_sldr_guard.py` (6 tests) pins the rule hermetically. **Bearing on this feature:** any census artifact captured before this fix may have been measured against a project whose writing systems were being quarantined mid-run - `src/gramtrans/Lib/flexinit.py`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/census.py`, `src/gramtrans/standalone/app.py`, `src/gramtrans/gramtrans.py`, `tests/integration/harness/full_run.py`

All five invocations were verified to parse against the shipped `build_parser()` (`capture-baseline`, `run --pre-transfer`, `run`, `gate --phase`, `diff`); no `--target` was reintroduced. ## Phase 4: US1 - Entries keep their grammatical analysis (P1)

**Goal**: identity-first matching with a governed natural-key fallback, so 2,088 MSAs stop vanishing and the starter phoneme inventory stops being duplicated 21 times over.

**Independent Test**: transfer a project containing entries with parts of speech into a freshly created empty FLEx project; count entries with a grammatical analysis in source and target. Delivers value alone - the receiving project has a usable lexicon even if nothing else ships.

**Verify against `Ejagham Mini` -> a freshly created disposable target. Never open `Target`** - 037 holds a live restore-bounded Move on `Projects\Target\Target.fwdata`.

## Phase 5: US4 - Existing destination items gain what they lack (P4)

**Goal**: an object matched by identity is enriched with the child collections its source counterpart has and it lacks, instead of being skipped whole.

**Independent Test**: transfer into a destination that already contains a part of speech which, in the source, owns slots, templates, features and sub-categories; confirm the destination's copy gains them.

**Depends on Phase 4.** Holds the `categories.py` claim for its duration (plan.md:289-290).

Reported as a distinct bucket rather than by reducing `add_created`, which is the honest answer to "how many creates were planned" and has three invariants checked against it: `add_write_failed` plus `add_created_written`, withheld as `None` when failures exceed planned creates. ## Phase 6: US5a - Affix process rules survive the transfer, wholly-owned graphs (P5)

**Goal**: a `MoAffixProcess` is reproduced with its input and output content, or reported and skipped - never downgraded into a plain allomorph.

**Independent Test**: transfer a project containing affix process rules and confirm each arrives with its input and output content intact.

**Depends on Phase 4 and on `038-affix-fidelity` having merged (T005).** Runs in parallel with Phase 5 except for `models.py` (T042 / T052) and the `categories.py` claim, which must be serialised between the two phases.

**Live corpus**: `Mbugwe LizzieHC practice` is the only project with rules - 18 `MoAffixProcess`, 124 `MoAffixAllomorph`, 137 `MoStemAllomorph`, `ContextsOS=93`, `FeatConstraintsOS=89`.

### Tests

- [X] **T048d** [US4] **A class that arrives correct with no record of arriving.** Raised by the 2026-08-20 re-run gate, and the **sole remaining blocker on T048's P3**: `MoMorphType` reports `difference -19` while the two `.fwdata` files hold the SAME 19 morph types by GUID (source 19, destination 19, 0 missing, 0 destination-only, identical GUID sets). This is NOT T048b's defect and T048b cannot reach it. `PartOfSpeech`'s starters were matched and the run RECORDED the match as an identity skip, which is what T048b reads; `MoMorphType` has no record of any kind -- no action, no overwrite, no skip -- because the morph-types list is project-independent fixed content that needs no transfer (`Lib/categories.py`, at the site that refuses to create one: "the morph-types list is project-independent fixed content, all 19 GUIDs byte-identical across the three projects measured"). So the class arrives correct, nothing happens, nothing is recorded, and the gross basis subtracts all 19 starters as surplus. Closing it needs evidence the census does not hold today: either a GUID-level comparison (the starter baseline records `class`, `count`, `names` and **no GUIDs**) or a declared "fixed canonical content, matched by construction" accounting line. Do NOT close it by attributing a match nothing recorded - `src/gramtrans/census_cli.py`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/census.py`. **DONE**: closed by the GUID-level comparison, the first of the two routes, but sourced from the two PROJECTS rather than from the baseline document -- the starter project (`GT038 T023b Scratch`) no longer exists on disk, so adding GUIDs to the baseline would have needed a re-capture that moves `content_hash` and invalidates every measurement already taken against it. `census.starter_matched_lower_bound` computes `B - |D \ Q|` clamped to `[0, B]`, a PROVABLE LOWER BOUND on `starter_matched_to_source` (proof in `census.py`'s section header), and a lower bound over-subtracts, so it can only ever manufacture a shortfall -- never hide one. Two guards carry the weight: the bound REFUSES when `|D| < B` (the arithmetic signature of its one assumption, `S subset of D`, having failed), and a CONCLUSIVENESS gate promotes a row to `baseline_matched` only when the audited difference is `>= 0`. That second guard is not cosmetic: promoting every audited row took the run verdict from `CENSUS_ACCOUNTED` to `UNEXPLAINED_SHORTFALL` on numbers that are UPPER bounds on the loss, which is 5.2's cap rationale reappearing one basis to the left. An unresolved audit keeps the gross basis and states what it narrowed the loss to in a note. Nothing reads a plan, a tally or a disposition, so no match is attributed. Measured live (`CENSUS-20260820-125529`): six phantom rows resolved to MATCHED -- `MoMorphType` 19/19, `LexEntryType` 11/11, `CmAgent` 4/4, `LexEntryInflType` 3/3, `PhBdryMarker` 2/2, `CmFolder` 1/1 -- and NO other row changed, run verdict `CENSUS_ACCOUNTED` before and after. P1/P2/P3/P4 all satisfied. 32 tests. See journal/T048d-identity-audit.md

- [X] **T049** [US5] Layer (a) - class-identity assertion on `_walk_entry_allomorphs` with a fake whose `ClassName == "MoAffixProcess"`: assert **neither** allomorph factory was invoked and that exactly one `DroppedItemRecord` with `item_name == "MoAffixProcess"` was appended - `tests/unit/test_038_process_rules.py`. **DONE** (`0a0071c`). The assertion is that neither factory was so much as **REQUESTED** (`target.GetFactory` is the spy point), which is strictly stronger than what `test_038_affix_fidelity.py` already had -- that file asserts nothing landed on the entry, and a create-then-discard satisfies it. Per the contract's layer (a), the create path is stubbed to **fail**, so a failed real create cannot fall back onto a simpler class. Three companions: the `AlternateFormsOS` field, an entry carrying a rule **beside** a good `MoAffixAllomorph` (the good one still transfers and the rule contributes exactly one record -- an entry losing its remaining allomorphs to one unreproducible member would be a second silent loss hiding behind the first), and Preview/Move drop-set parity through `_plan_entry_reference_decisions` (Principle III).

This is what makes the downgrade unable to recur for a class nobody thought of - `tests/unit/test_038_process_rules.py`. **DONE** (`0a0071c`). Parametrised over eleven refused names: `MoAffixProcess`, a plausible future subclass, two real LCM neighbours that are not `IMoForm` (`MoInflAffixSlot`/`MoInflAffixTemplate`), `MoDerivAffMsa`, `PhSequenceContext`, `LexEntry`, wrong case, trailing space, `""` and `None`. Each case also asserts the refusal is **reported** -- a silent refusal is the same loss by a different mechanism. - [X] **T051** [US5] Write the failing invariant that **no `PlannedAction` may name a target class differing from its source class** (data-model.md:191-196) - under no circumstance is an object created as a simpler kind - `tests/unit/test_038_process_rules.py`. **DONE** (`0a0071c`). `PlannedAction` carries no class field and T052 does not add one, so the invariant is tested where a class is actually **chosen**, in two halves: (1) the dispatch never RENAMES -- `_dispatch_allomorph_subclass(s)` is `None` or exactly `s`, over the whole corpus; (2) the object actually created for a dispatchable class is of that class, driven through the real `_walk_entry_allomorphs` with the factory request and the created class both spied. A third test covers the plan side: Preview emits no reference decision naming a rule Move refuses, since decisions naming an object that will never exist are how a plan comes to name a class the run cannot deliver. **Stated plainly: these are green on landing, not red.** The destructive half was already fixed on `038-affix-fidelity` (`18c0ece`), so there is nothing left to fail against today -- what they are is the trap T057 exists to avoid, and that trap is **mutation-verified**: whitelisting `MoAffixProcess` while the factory ternary still routes every non-stem subclass to `IMoAffixAllomorphFactory` fails with `source MoAffixProcess was created as ['MoAffixAllomorph']`, the historic defect reproduced by name. The harness reads the whitelist at run time and derives each factory's class from its interface NAME (`I<Class>Factory`), so `MoAffixProcess` joins the invariant the moment T057 lands -- with no edit to this file, and no way to add it silently.

- [x] **T090** [US3] **A driver run silently converts live integration coverage into skips.** Raised by T069's test runs 2026-08-21. `debug/audit038_closure_edges.py` and `debug/run038_closure_census.py` open their source project through `harness.full_run._open_source_readonly` and leave a **`<project>.fwdata.lock`** file behind when the process exits (measured: `{"__type":"FileLockContent:#Palaso.IO.FileLock","PID":32284,"ProcessName":"python",...}` for a PID no longer running). `tests/integration/test_038_phon_empty_drop_live.py::_open_or_skip` treats the presence of that file as "locked by FieldWorks; a locked project is a refusal, not a measurement" and **skips** -- which is the right call for a genuinely locked project and the wrong outcome for a stale file. **Measured effect**: with the two locks present (`Mbugwe LizzieHC practice`, `Ejagham Mini`) `tests/integration` reports **77 skipped**; after removing them, **75 skipped and 2 more passed** -- four live assertions across two parametrisations. So running a Phase 7 driver makes the NEXT suite run quieter, in a way that reads as green. This is the feature's recurring shape once more (something that exists and is read at a level where it cannot do its job), on the harness rather than on the engine. **Two fixes, and the second is not optional**: (a) have the drivers close their project (or use a context manager) so the lock is released -- `_open_source_readonly` currently has no paired close, and a read-only open taking a write lock is worth checking against flexicon before assuming the driver is at fault; and (b) make `_open_or_skip` distinguish a STALE lock from a live one by checking whether the recorded PID is running, and **report rather than skip** when it is stale -- a skip whose cause is a dead process is a measurement this repo declined to make for no reason. **Do not close (b) by deleting lock files in a fixture**: a fixture that removes a lock cannot tell a stale lock from a live FLEx session, and stealing a lock from a running FieldWorks is exactly the blast radius CLAUDE.md's restore-before-write rule exists to avoid - `tests/integration/test_038_phon_empty_drop_live.py`, `tests/integration/harness/full_run.py`, `debug/audit038_closure_edges.py`, `debug/run038_closure_census.py` **CLOSED 2026-08-22** (`f0801a9` on `038-transfer-fidelity-gaps`). **FLEXICON IS NOT AT FAULT AND THE TASK WAS RIGHT TO ASK.** Checked through FLExToolsMCP before touching a driver: `FLExProject.OpenProject`'s own docstring says the project "must be closed with `CloseProject()` to save any changes, **AND RELEASE THE LOCK**", with NO read-only exemption -- the lock is taken on ANY open, so the leak is the caller's. **`run038_closure_census.py` was already clean**: it reaches a project only through `run_full_transfer` (closes both handles in a `finally`) and `census_cli` -> `census.read_project` (same); its locks came from the audit driver sharing its default `SOURCE = "Mbugwe LizzieHC practice"`. **(a)** `audit038_closure_edges.py` had no paired close AND no `try`/`finally`, so the success and exception paths both leaked; it now opens through a new `full_run.source_readonly` context manager with its body in `_audit(proj)`. The sweep over 30 project-touching files in `debug/` found **exactly one sibling**, `probe_adhoc_loss.py` (source AND target, neither closed), now closing both in nested `finally`s; a test pins the CLASS -- no file in `debug/` may open a project unless the same file closes it, delegates to `run_full_transfer`/`census.read_project`, or uses `source_readonly`. **(b)** `full_run.read_project_lock` classifies ABSENT/HELD/STALE/UNREADABLE from whether the recorded PID is running; `_open_or_skip` **and `_live_project_or_skip`** -- the SECOND site, found by the same sweep, in `test_object_census.py:3928` -- refuse on HELD/UNREADABLE and report-then-measure on STALE. **The rule is deliberately ASYMMETRIC and that is the design**: calling a live lock stale would have flexicon overwrite the lock file with our PID and delete it on close, stealing it from a running FieldWorks, so STALE requires a PID that is DEFINITIVELY gone and "running" / "cannot tell" / "no PID" / "unreadable" all refuse. **NOTHING DELETES A LOCK FILE** and nothing needs to -- flexicon replaces a stale file on open and removes it on close, observed directly. **`os.kill(pid, 0)` is unusable here**: CPython implements os.kill on Windows through TerminateProcess, so the POSIX liveness idiom would terminate the process it asks about; `_pid_is_running` uses OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION) + GetExitCodeProcess, with ACCESS_DENIED reading as alive. A recycled PID is left undecided ON PURPOSE -- matching `ProcessName` would move a case from HELD to STALE, the one direction that must not be guessed at, and getting it wrong can only make the suite quieter, never less safe. **MEASURED LIVE on `Ejagham Mini`**, `test_038_phon_empty_drop_live.py`: no lock 12 passed; **stale lock planted -> 12 passed** with the `[WARN] ... STALE ... measuring anyway` line (before: 10 passed / 2 skipped); **live lock planted (PID 4, FieldWorks) -> 10 passed / 2 skipped and the file UNTOUCHED**. `audit038_closure_edges.py` re-run end to end on that project exits 0, leaves **no lock**, and reproduces the committed `closure-edge-audit-038-ejagham-mini.json` BYTE FOR BYTE (its six verdicts also agree relationship-for-relationship with the Mbugwe corpus). 30 tests in `tests/integration/test_038_stale_lock.py`, every one running against `tmp_path` so they pin on a host with no FieldWorks. The four pre-existing stale locks on disk (`Ejagham Full GT-Test`, `Ejagham Full`, `Esperanto`, `Hdi`, three dead PIDs) were LEFT IN PLACE -- the classifier reads all four correctly, and deleting other people's lock files is not this task's business. This session left no new ones. See journal/T090-T103-a-lock-is-a-claim-about-a-process.md

NOT a fifth missed T033 site: until the plan could remap a POS the resolver had nothing to do there. **Run 2 still lost all 12, and the cause is T088's defect class verbatim**: the same function computed `IPartOfSpeech(src_owner)` purely as a raises-if-not-a-POS probe and THREW THE RESULT AWAY. `Owner` is declared `ICmObject`, pythonnet resolves attributes against the STATIC wrapper type, so `Name` is invisible on the uncast proxy -- `ClassName` IS on `ICmObject` so the subclass check passed and the object was merely UNKEYABLE, a miss that reads as "no such category". Keeping the cast is the fix; run 3 resolves all 12. **WHY IT IS STILL NOT A CLOSURE: the duplicate was LOAD-BEARING.** `total_shortfall` 70638 -> 72528 and four MSA classes fall out of MATCHED (`MoStemMsa` 1951 -> 164, `MoInflAffMsa` 134 -> 36, `MoDerivAffMsa` 3 -> 0, `MoUnclassifiedAffixMsa` 2 -> 0), because **1,848** MSAs point at one of the five now-remapped POSes and `categories.py:8602` resolves `MoStemMsa.PartOfSpeechRA`/`MoInflAffMsa.PartOfSpeechRA` with the TWO-POSITIONAL `_resolve_target_pos(target, pg)` -- the call shape `test_two_positional_call_never_consults_the_key` pins as "exactly its pre-038 behaviour", because T032 landed the fallback ahead of its call-site sweep and T033's sweep stopped at four sites. Five remain; filed as **T094**. The run did NOT get less honest -- `unexplained_shortfall` is byte-identical at 68920, `accounted_shortfall` 0 -> 1890 carries the whole delta, and every loss is a `dropped_items` record naming class, field and POS GUID -- but 1,848 senses losing their part-of-speech analysis to remove 5 duplicate objects is the trade backwards. **`LexEntryInflType` IS COVERED AND ITS EFFECT IS UNMEASURED**: same insertion, and on both pairs its row already had 0 duplicate groups, so nothing in the corpus exercises it. Its only evidence is three unit tests. 17 unit tests added to `tests/unit/test_038_pos_natural_key_fallback.py` (7 bodies parameterized over both POS planners); reverting `categories.py` alone turns 7 red. `test_017_gold_reserved_edit_copy.py:348` re-verified and holds unchanged (empty candidate scope) -- no tripwire edit needed anywhere. `test_038_report_natural_key.py:199` finally has a producer. tests/unit 3471 -> 3488 passed, **79 skipped both sides**; tests/integration 397 passed / **75 skipped both sides**, the 1 failure (`Ngoreme FLEx` digest drift) reproducing at `c738b47`. Drivers left no new `.fwdata.lock` (same four as before). The row predicate held THROUGH the sweep -- `census.row_passes` on Ngoreme's `PartOfSpeech` still True, 26 -> 26, `difference_raw` 0, duplicate groups 0, extra objects 0, `starter_matched_to_source` 5, `identity_substitution.per_category["GRAM_CATEGORIES"] == 5`, `matched_to_source.by_object_class["PartOfSpeech"] == 5` -- and the four MSA classes came back to their pre-T091 values TO THE OBJECT: `MoStemMsa` 164 -> **1951**, `MoInflAffMsa` 36 -> **134**, `MoDerivAffMsa` 0 -> **3**, `MoUnclassifiedAffixMsa` 0 -> **2**, all four MATCHED. `total_shortfall` 72528 -> **70638**, the pre-T091 baseline to the unit; `unexplained_shortfall` byte-identical at **68920** on all three runs; `accounted_shortfall` 1890 -> **0**, so the 1,848 `dropped_items` records are GONE rather than re-labelled. `duplicate_extra_objects` 26 -> 21: the five POS duplicates removed and STAYING removed, which is the point -- T094 was not allowed to buy the MSAs back by putting the duplicates back. `Ejagham W Mini` byte-identical on all three runs, 0 rows moved. So the seam is **bypassed, not unwanted**: deleting it would ratify the divergence its own docblock exists to end, and one of the four has already lost the seam's enumeration-failure report *and inverted its ambiguity contract* — an ambiguous key reaches the operator at one site and is silently "unresolved" at the other. **Decision: the seam earns its callers.** The routing is **T105**, not this task, exactly as this filing required: it is a live-behaviour change needing its own census, and **only two of the four bypasses can reach the seam at all** (the seam takes a `RunContext`; two sites hold none). - [X] **T105** [US1] **Route the four plan-time match bypasses through `preview.plan_match_decision`, or record per site why not.** Raised 2026-08-22 by T092, which measured the bypasses and deliberately did not route them. **THE MEASUREMENT IS DONE AND PINNED** — `tests/unit/test_038_plan_match_decision.py` names the five production `resolve_match` sites and fails on a sixth, so this task starts from a fact about the tree rather than a survey. **WHY IT IS ITS OWN TASK**: routing is a live-behaviour change across every category that plans a roster-admitted class and needs its own census, per T092's own filing. **THE SCOPE SPLITS TWO WAYS, AND THE SPLIT IS THE INTERESTING PART.** (a) `_process_referent_by_natural_key` and `_plan_natural_key_match` hold a `RunContext` and can be routed today — but the first **swallows `NaturalKeyAmbiguityError`** where the seam propagates it, and *both readings are argued for in their own docstrings*: the seam says "an ambiguous key is a harness error the operator must see", the site says "guessing between two same-named destination phonemes is how a rule silently comes to match the wrong segment". The referent leg keeps its absorb, now visible as an `except` at the call site rather than implied by a divergent copy of the seam's body, and it wins on measured grounds (T098: 12 duplicate-name groups in `PhNCFeatures` on Ngoreme, 1 on Ejagham; T043: 21 duplicate phoneme names in one destination -- propagating would abort runs over the common case). `_plan_natural_key_match` propagates, agreeing with the seam. **No census was required and none was faked**: both routed sites already reproduced the seam's wiring (one line for line, one via `candidates=`), and the dropped `_unwrap_lcm` is a no-op because `ObjectsIn` returns `iter(repo.AllInstances())` -- checked in flexicon, not assumed. - [x] **T093** [US3] **An `IncompletenessRecord` is emitted for a deselected dependency that is ALREADY IN THE DESTINATION, where nothing is incomplete.** Raised 2026-08-21 by T073's live census, and PINNED as current defective behaviour (`tests/integration/test_038_closure_edge_audit.py::test_the_over_report_this_task_deliberately_did_not_fix` asserts the number, so closing this has to be a deliberate edit and not silent drift). **Measured** (`tests/integration/_snapshots/incompleteness-038-t073.json`, key `T093_dependencies_already_in_the_destination`; `Mbugwe LizzieHC practice` -> throwaway restored from `Target 2026-07-06 0218.fwbackup`, AFFIX_TEMPLATES-only): **2 of the 5** pulled-in POSes already exist in the destination -- T070 measured them as OVERWRITEs rather than ADDs, and those two GUIDs are recorded in the snapshot -- so when the user deselects one, the dependent's reference still resolves against the object that is already there and the dependent is NOT incomplete. **8 of T073's 35 records name one of those two**, i.e. ~23% of the reported incompletenesses under a full deselection are over-reports. This is the OPPOSITE direction from Principle I's silent loss and it is not the safer one: CLAUDE.md records the same shape for flexicon 4.5.1's natural-class features, where a source-aware guard "reports a loss that did not happen" and 3 phantom losses were what made 4.5.1 unshippable. A report that cries loss where there was none teaches the reader to stop reading it. **Why T073 cannot tell, and why the fix is not a one-liner**: T071 suppresses a deselected ref BEFORE its planner runs -- which is what makes "a deselected dependency is not planned" true, is what its own live measurement asserts (composition returns to exactly the registry-emptied shape), and is correct for the plan -- so no `ALREADY_PRESENT_BY_GUID` / `_BY_IDENTITY` skip exists for `_plan_incompleteness` to consult, and `_DEPENDENCY_PRESENT_SKIPS` therefore cannot fire on the deselected path at all. Two candidate repairs, both needing their own evidence: (a) run the refused ref's `plan_action` purely to learn its match outcome, then discard everything but the presence fact -- this CHANGES T071's measured composition (an `ALREADY_PRESENT` skip appears where a `DEPENDENCY_DESELECTED` skip stood) and breaks `test_a_deselected_dependency_is_reported_not_dropped`, so it needs a decision about which skip a refused-but-present dependency should carry; (b) add a target-presence probe -- but a bare GUID-presence check is **Defect G3's exact shape** (`ALREADY_PRESENT_BY_GUID` taken without a field-identity comparison), the premise this feature exists to remove, so it needs an audit establishing that "can this reference resolve" is the one question GUID presence legitimately answers. Same reasoning that kept T089's re-pointing out of a registration and T092's routing out of T091. **Do not close this by loosening the pinned assertion.** **CLOSED 2026-08-24 (commit `4ec8fff`).** Neither candidate repair as written. (a)'s stated consequence -- an `ALREADY_PRESENT` skip standing where a `DEPENDENCY_DESELECTED` one stood -- follows only if the planner's `Skip` is appended to `skips`, and it does not have to be: the probe runs the refused ref's own `plan_action`, reads the verdict and DISCARDS the result, so no plan member and no skip move and the live deselection skips are the same `{gram_categories: 5, slots: 18}` before and after. (b)'s objection stands and is answered by not doing it -- the question is put to the category's own identity-aware matcher, never to `guid in target`. The presence test also had to move ABOVE the cause ladder: `deselected > cycle > unsatisfiable` orders EXPLANATIONS for an incompleteness and cannot decide whether there is one. **MEASURED** (`tests/integration/_snapshots/incompleteness-038-t093.json`, `python debug/run038_incompleteness_census.py T093`, same pair, same backup, preview-only): P1 **35 -> 27** records with `35 - 27 == 8` exactly the over-reports, over-reports **8 -> 0**, P2 **29 -> 7**, P0 and P3 still 0, deselection skips unchanged. P2 is the reading that matters and the filing understated it -- the 2 already-present POSes were the dependency of 22 of its 29 records, so three quarters of the selection a user is likeliest to make was phantom. T073's artifact is NOT overwritten (T102's drift); the driver refuses to re-measure it in place and the integration block now separates what the code DOES from what it USED TO do. See journal/T093-the-refusal-that-was-reported-as-a-loss.md - `src/gramtrans/Lib/preview.py`

- [x] **T095** [US1] **T091 ended the invariant "a category's destination GUID is its source GUID", and four GUID-only target-POS lookups outside `_resolve_target_pos` still assume it -- one of them says so in its own docstring.** Raised 2026-08-21 by T094's live run on `Ngoreme FLEx` (`debug/run038_before_after_pairs.py`; artifacts `tests/integration/_snapshots/census-038-t094-ngoreme.json`, `_run_reports/038-after-ngoreme-report.json`). **THE MEASUREMENT.** After T094's sweep `total_shortfall` is back to the pre-T091 70638 exactly and `accounted_shortfall` is 0 -- and **two** `Skip(DEPENDENCY_UNRESOLVED)` remain on `GRAM_CATEGORIES`: `pos_guid=c46c8242-... not in target after categories+features transfer` (Ngoreme's `Noun`, one of the five categories T091 reuses) and the same for `ff5c5e07-...`. **THE MECHANISM, and its own docstring is the filing.** `_run_infl_feature_link_pass` (`categories.py:9454`) resolves the POS endpoint of `plan.feature_category_links` with `_resolve_target_by_guid`, a plain GUID lookup, and `_stash_feature_category_links` (`:4800-4809`) records the key with the premise written out: *"Records `{target_pos_guid: [feature_guid, ...]}` -- GUIDs are preserved on transfer so target_pos_guid == source pos guid."* That sentence was true until T091 and is the whole defect. Two of the five reused categories therefore lose their `InflectableFeatsRC` wiring. **WHY IT NEEDS A FILING RATHER THAN A CENSUS ROW, and this is the interesting part.** It is REPORTED (a Skip, never silent) and it costs **0** shortfall, because `InflectableFeatsRC` membership is a reference collection and not a counted object class -- so no census row moves, `total_shortfall` reads clean, and the loss is real. A predicate scoped to counted classes cannot see this, which is T087's blind spot one layer out. **THREE MORE SITES SHARE THE SHAPE**, all open-coded GUID-only scans over `target.POS.GetAll(recursive=True)` that `return None` on a miss: `stem_names_execute_action` (`categories.py:~3174`), `exception_features_plan_action` (`~3283`) and `exception_features_execute_action` (`~3332`). None of them is a `_resolve_target_pos` call site, which is exactly why T094's sweep and its structural pin do not reach them -- the pin greps for the resolver, and an open-coded scan is invisible to it. **LATENT ON THIS PAIR, NOT LATENT IN GENERAL**: Ngoreme's `MoStemName` row is `0 -> 0` so the stem-name site has nothing to lose today, and no census row is bound to `ExceptionFeaturesOC`; a corpus with stem names under a starter-named category would lose them silently-except-for-a-Skip. **THE FIX** is to route all four through `_resolve_target_pos` with the keywords (or, for `_run_infl_feature_link_pass`, to stash the PLANNED destination GUID via `planned_destination_for` instead of asserting the two are equal) and to widen T094's structural pin so an open-coded target-POS scan fails it. **NOT foldable into T094**: three more categories' live behaviour, and T094's own predicate is a `total_shortfall` this defect does not move -- so it needs a different instrument, which is the reasoning that kept T092 out of T091 and T094 out of T091 in turn. Tenth appearance of this feature's recurring shape, and the first where the thing read at the wrong level is a stated ASSUMPTION rather than a value. See journal/T094-the-sweep-that-had-no-producer.md - `src/gramtrans/Lib/categories.py` **CLOSED 2026-08-24 (commit `a2c32bf`).** All four routed through one new helper, `_target_pos_for_source_guid` (identity first, roster-admitted natural key second, source object fetched when the caller holds only a GUID), placed beside `_resolve_target_pos` so a site swept later inherits it. `_run_infl_feature_link_pass` keeps its object-repository hit AHEAD of it -- that is what makes the change additive for a run where every GUID was preserved, and it is the contract the offline fakes implement. `stem_names_execute_action` had the owner object in hand and kept only its GUID; it now keeps both, which is T094's `_pos_guid_of` finding at a second site. **MEASURED** (`Ngoreme FLEx` -> `GT038 Ngoreme After` restored from `Target 2026-07-06 0218.fwbackup`, same branch and selection; evidence committed as `tests/integration/_snapshots/skips-038-t095-ngoreme.json` because `_run_reports/` is gitignored and this task's own filing cited a report in the since-removed `038-t091` worktree): the two GRAM_CATEGORIES `DEPENDENCY_UNRESOLVED` skips go **2 -> 0**, and the rest of the report is identical -- 20 AFFIXES skips, `leaf_failed` 11, identity_substitution 26 with GRAM_CATEGORIES 5 on both sides, so the run always knew where those two categories were. Census `total_shortfall` 70638 -> 70646 and **none of the +8 is this task's**: +11 source drift (`Ngoreme FLEx` has drifted again -- WfiWordform +10, PunctuationForm +1 among short classes) less 3 improvements from the process-rule tasks landed between the runs; `PartOfSpeech` did not move and `duplicate_extra_objects` stayed 21, so T091's clause is still closed. Ejagham byte-identical to T094's on every total (4781 / 3063 / 53 matched / 19 short / 3 duplicate extra). **THE PIN IS WIDENED, NOT REUSED**: `test_no_unclassified_open_coded_target_pos_scan_exists` enumerates every `target.POS.GetAll(...)` in production by AST, addressed by enclosing FUNCTION rather than line number (this task's own filing named line numbers that had drifted ~90 lines), and requires each to carry a reason. Re-deriving the count the way T094 re-derived its own found **seven more** finders of the same shape outside `categories.py` -- filed as T106. Reverting `categories.py` alone turns 11 of the 16 new tests red. Two deliberate pin edits: T101's artifact count 7 -> 9 (its load-bearing clause untouched), and the evidence committed as an artifact rather than cited by path. See journal/T095-the-invariant-that-was-written-down.md

- [x] **T106** [US1] **Seven more target-POS finders share T095's shape, and their callers mix source GUIDs with destination ones.** Raised 2026-08-24 by T095, whose widened structural pin (`tests/unit/test_038_t095_target_pos_scan_sweep.py::test_no_unclassified_open_coded_target_pos_scan_exists`) enumerates every `target.POS.GetAll(...)` in production by AST -- the instrument T094's pin could not be, because it greps for `_resolve_target_pos` and an open-coded scan does not call it. **THE COUNT, RE-DERIVED**: ten scans, of which the three left in `categories.py` are genuinely not resolutions (the natural-key candidate scope; the verb-vertical *did THIS RUN already create this GUID* guard; a walk over every category's `StemNamesOC` for a stem-name GUID). The other seven are `transfer._find_target_pos_by_guid` (**six callers**, some passing a destination `tgt_guid` and some a source `owner_pos_guid`/`src_verb_guid`), `transfer._execute_overwrite`'s SLOTS fallback, `preview._find_pos_by_guid` and `preview._target_has_pos_guid` (two callers each), `merge_preview._find_target_pos_by_guid` / `_find_target_gram_cat_by_guid`, and `conflict._find_target_pos_by_guid`. **WHY IT IS A FILING AND NOT PART OF T095**: the fix is not uniform. A caller that already holds the plan's `target_guid` is CORRECT as it stands and routing it through the natural key would re-derive a decision Preview already made (Principle III); a caller holding a source GUID has T095's defect exactly. Separating them is a read of thirteen call sites across four modules, and `preview._target_has_pos_guid` needs a further decision -- whether its callers mean *is this GUID present* (a legitimate identity question, T093's distinction) or *is this category present* (the defect). **LATENT UNTIL MEASURED**: T095's live pair shows no loss from any of the seven, so this needs its own instrument rather than a re-run -- the same reasoning that kept T092 out of T091 and T094 out of T091 in turn. Each is allowlisted in the pin WITH THIS TASK NAMED, so the debt is visible rather than absent; **do not close this by deleting the allowlist entries** - `src/gramtrans/Lib/transfer.py`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/preview.py`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/merge_preview.py`, `src/gramtrans/Lib/conflict.py` **CLOSED 2026-08-25 (commit `2482a53`).** **THE COUNT, RE-DERIVED ONCE MORE**: the filing said thirteen call sites; the tree holds **sixteen**. Five are **CORRECT_AS_IS** and were deliberately left identity-only -- `_execute_overwrite`'s POS branch (`overwrite.target_guid`) and its SLOTS fallback (walks POS for a SLOT guid), `merge_preview`'s `pos` / `gram_cat` table entries (`props_for` is called once per SIDE, so neither is a cross-project lookup), and `conflict.collect_overwrite_conflicts` (`ow.target_guid`, the same value it uses as a destination identity key two lines down). Eight were **DEFECTIVE and are swept** through new `_target_pos_for_source_guid` wrappers in `transfer.py` and `preview.py`, each mirroring `owned._resolve_target_pos_by_guid` (lazy import, keyword-only `src_pos`/`source_handle`, both defaulting to None so the change is additive). **BOTH keywords are required** for the key -- it is the category's `Name` AND it is scoped to the source writing systems -- which is precisely why `merge_preview._find_gap_object` was **not** swept: no source handle exists at that call site, so a `src_pos` alone would merely LOOK fixed, and its miss is already absorbed by a fallback that finds the template by its own GUID (now pinned, because deleting it as dead would turn a slow path into a blank pane). `merge_preview._find_target_template_by_guid` is **unreachable** (`is_gap=True` short-circuits `props_for` before any finder dispatch) and is documented rather than swept, since a sweep there would be unverifiable. **THE ONE LIVE, SILENT DEFECT** was `_execute_overwrite`'s MSA branch: it resolved the owning category from `src_ia.PartOfSpeechRA` (a SOURCE object) to rebuild `SlotsRC`, and on a miss the whole re-sync sat behind `if tgt_pos is not None:` with **no else** -- no Warning, no Skip, no `DroppedItemRecord`, the MSA quietly keeping its old slot membership, and `SlotsRC` being a reference collection rather than a counted class means **no census row moves and `total_shortfall` reads clean either way**. T087's blind spot at a third site and Principle I's exact shape. It now routes through the key AND reports the residual miss, guarded on a non-empty source `SlotsRC` so an MSA with nothing to sync cannot manufacture a phantom loss (the flexicon 4.5.1 shape CLAUDE.md records). **WHY THE OTHER SEVEN WERE LATENT, which the filing did not know**: six sit behind `_VERB_VERTICAL_ENABLED = False` in *both* `transfer.py` and `preview.py` -- dead in production today, live the moment anybody flips the flag to A/B the legacy path. That is why T095's live pair showed no loss from any of them, and it is why this task needed **its own instrument rather than a re-run** (the reasoning that kept T092 out of T091). The gate is now pinned in both modules. Their direction is also the OPPOSITE of T095's: a probe answering 'absent' for a category present under another GUID makes the planner emit a **duplicate**, and makes `_check_msa_pos_excluded_lossy` warn *'Entry X will have no Part of Speech'* about a link that resolves fine. **THE INSTRUMENT** is `tests/unit/test_038_t106_target_pos_finder_provenance.py` (24 tests), which pins **both directions** -- that the swept sites resolve by natural key AND that the correct-as-is sites stay identity-pure, because a later 'tidy-up' sweep of the latter is the way this classification gets lost. Reverting `transfer.py` and `preview.py` alone turns **17 of the 24** red. **TWO DELIBERATE PIN EDITS, neither a relaxation**: T095's allowlist is **reworded, not trimmed** -- the task's own instruction -- so every entry still names a finder that is the right question for at least one caller, the scan count is unchanged at **10**, and the `>= 10` floor holds (it sat exactly at the current count, so any removal would have tripped it); and T094's `test_the_pin_can_see_every_site_the_filing_named` file set gains `transfer.py` + `preview.py`, while its load-bearing sibling (no production call site is two-positional) covers both new wrappers unchanged. Full unit suite **3693 passed / 0 failed**; hermetic census gate **291 passed**.

- [x] **T107** [US5] **`PhSimpleContextBdry` is held behind `_PROCESS_UNEXERCISED_CLASSES` on a premise `Ejagham W Mini` falsifies, and it is the sole blocker of 13 of the corpus's 32 affix process rules.** Raised 2026-08-25 by T078's post-037 re-census, which measured the process-rule path on all three sanctioned pairs rather than on the one T076 had. **THE NUMBERS** (`tests/integration/_snapshots/process-rules-038-t078-corpus.json`, derived read-only from the run reports the T078 censuses judge): corpus-wide **32 `MoAffixProcess` rules, 19 reproduced, 13 not**, and `PhSimpleContextBdry` is the **only** class named in any skip reason. Per corpus: `Mbugwe LizzieHC practice` 18/18, `Ngoreme FLEx` 1/1, **`Ejagham W Mini` 13 rules / 0 reproduced** -- 8 blocked with the boundary context as a DIRECT input member, 5 through a `PhSequenceContext` in `PhPhonData.ContextsOS` that references one, i.e. through exactly the shared-context route T076 built. Census row: `MoAffixProcess` **13 -> 0 SHORTFALL** on the Ejagham pair, MATCHED on the other two. **WHY T076 WAS NOT WRONG**: its measurement -- Mbugwe holds 22 `PhSimpleContextBdry` and 11 `PhIterationContext` in `ContextsOS` and not one is referenced by any of its 18 rules -- is correct and still reproduces. What does not survive is the inference drawn from it, "admitting them would ship a create path no corpus can check", because it was taken on one corpus. `PhIterationContext` keeps the original reason intact and is NOT part of this task. **SCOPE**: admit `PhSimpleContextBdry` to `_PROCESS_INPUT_FACTORIES`' co-create path and to `_PROCESS_SHARED_CONTEXT_CLASSES`, wiring `FeatureStructureRA` to the destination's `PhBdryMarker` by the same resolvability test T076 landed for phonemes and natural classes (`PhBdryMarker` is 2 -> 2 MATCHED on all three pairs, so the referent exists to resolve against) -- and a context whose boundary marker does NOT resolve must still skip, exactly as T076 requires. **IT NEEDS ITS OWN LIVE CENSUS**, not a re-run of T078's: this changes what transfers, so the acceptance is a restored-target run on the Ejagham pair showing `MoAffixProcess` 13 -> 13 with `PhSequenceContext`/`PhSimpleContextBdry` moving by an accounted delta, plus a re-run on Mbugwe proving 18/18 is unmoved. **THE DROP-REASON STRING IS PART OF THIS TASK.** The live reason reads "a class with zero instances in any sanctioned corpus" and every sanctioned source holds the class -- 10 (Ejagham), 13 (Ngoreme), 24 (Mbugwe). T078 pinned those three numbers (`test_the_skip_reason_the_engine_emits_is_now_factually_wrong`) and deliberately did not touch the string: it is live run-report output for four classes and a reporting change with no census behind it. Fix it here, or hand it to T079. **DO NOT CLOSE THIS BY WIDENING `_PROCESS_UNEXERCISED_CLASSES`' COMMENT** -- T078 already corrected the comment; what is owed is the path - `src/gramtrans/Lib/categories.py`, `tests/integration/test_038_process_rules.py` **DONE 2026-08-25, and the acceptance figure is 13 -> 12, not 13 -> 13 -- because there was a second blocker behind the first.** **WHAT LANDED**: `PhSimpleContextBdry` left `_PROCESS_UNEXERCISED_CLASSES` and joined `_PROCESS_INPUT_FACTORIES` (`IPhSimpleContextBdryFactory`) and `_PROCESS_SHARED_CONTEXT_CLASSES`. `IPhSimpleContextBdry` carries the same single `FeatureStructureRA` as the Seg and NC contexts (verified against the LCM index via FLExToolsMCP), so it adds no new shape. **THE TWO ROUTES SPLIT EXACTLY, verified read-only against the source graph BEFORE a line was written**: of Ejagham's 10 `PhSimpleContextBdry`, **8 are owned by a `MoAffixProcess`** (direct `InputOS`), **1 by `PhPhonData`** (reached by 5 distinct rules through a rule-owned `PhSequenceContext`) and **1 by a `PhSegRuleRHS`** (a phonological rule, NOT US5's). 8 + 5 = the 13 blocked rules, no rule counted twice. **THE REFERENT NEEDS NO CLOSURE EDGE, and that is measured**: all three sanctioned sources AND both live destinations hold the same two `PhBdryMarker` GUIDs -- `3bde17ce-...cb56` and `7db635e0-...89aaa`, LCM's fixed word/morpheme markers -- every one of the 10 contexts points at one of them, and the census reads `PhBdryMarker` 2 -> 2 MATCHED on all three pairs, so the identity leg resolves. **`_PROCESS_SIMPLE_CONTEXT_CLASSES` IS THE PART THAT WAS NEARLY A BUG**: the resolvability check was an inline ("PhSimpleContextSeg", "PhSimpleContextNC") tuple, so giving a third class a factory without editing that tuple would have created boundary contexts with NULL REFERENTS and reported all 13 rules as successful transfers -- trading a reported loss for an unreported one, strictly worse than the skip it replaced. It is now DERIVED from `_PROCESS_CONTEXT_REFERENT_KIND`, at both sites that spelled it inline (the second, `_entry_process_rule_deps._add_context`, is deliberately a no-op -- `_PROCESS_REFERENT_CATEGORY` has no `PhBdryMarker` -- derived anyway so the NEXT context class gets its closure edge without a second edit). **THE DROP-REASON STRING IS FIXED, and not the way this task described**: deferring it to T107 paid, because "a class with zero instances in any sanctioned corpus" is ALSO false of `PhIterationContext` (Mbugwe holds 11 in `ContextsOS`; a live run created 9 more under transferred phonological rules), so deleting the one false case would have left a false sentence standing. The claim is now the per-RULE one every remaining member satisfies: "a class **no affix process rule in any sanctioned corpus uses**". **THE LIVE EVIDENCE** (`census-038-t107-ejagham.json`, `process-rules-038-t107-ejagham.json`, from a full transfer into `GT038 Ejagham After` restored from `backups/Target 2026-07-06 0218.fwbackup` first): **EXACTLY 5 of 74 rows moved** -- `MoAffixProcess` -13 -> **-1**, `PhSequenceContext` -40 -> **-6**, **`PhSimpleContextBdry` -9 -> 0 MATCHED (10/10)**, `PhSimpleContextNC` -38 -> **-2**, `PhSimpleContextSeg` -27 -> **-1**; `total_shortfall` 4781 -> 4664 and `unexplained_shortfall` 3063 -> 2946, the SAME -117 both, so nothing moved into an accounting line; the other 69 rows identical, `PhCode` -43 included. **THE DELTA IS ACCOUNTED OBJECT BY OBJECT** by a read-only GUID diff of the two `.fwdata` files, not inferred from counts: `MoAffixProcess` -1 is the one rule below; `PhSequenceContext` -6 is 5 owned by that rule plus 1 owned by `PhSegRuleRHS < PhRegularRule < PhPhonData`; `PhSimpleContextNC` -2 and `PhSimpleContextSeg` -1 are all owned DIRECTLY by `PhPhonData`, shared-pool contexts no affix process rule reaches. **THE 13TH RULE IS NOT THIS CLASS.** Rule `24ed706a-7df2-4609-a37b-2bfa28853ccc` is refused because its `OutputOS[0]` is a `MoCopyFromInput` whose `Content` is **EMPTY IN THE SOURCE** -- verified in the `.fwdata`, a copy step with nothing to copy, and the FR-023 refusal is correct and pre-existing. `_resolve_process_graph` checks input members BEFORE output steps, so the boundary block fired first on all 13 and masked it: T078's "the ONLY blocking class anywhere" was true of the reasons the ENGINE EMITTED, which is all a skip reason can report -- one blocker per rule, the first one found. **A single-blocker census is a LOWER BOUND on the work, never a count of it.** 13 -> 13 was never reachable by admitting a class; nothing was bent to reach it. **MBUGWE IS UNMOVED TO THE ROW**: 18/18 reproduced, `MoAffixProcess` delta 18, `MoAffixAllomorph` delta 124, and **0 of 74 census rows differ** with every total identical to T078's (`census-038-t107-mbugwe-phase6.json`, `process-rules-038-t107-mbugwe.json`). Its `PhSimpleContextBdry` -15 is unmoved and CORRECT -- no affix process rule reaches those, which is T076's measurement standing. **NGOREME WAS NOT RE-MEASURED** and the roster reason says so: its single rule already reproduced, so nothing T107 changed can reach that pair. **SECOND FINDING -- T076's SC-010 FIELD NEVER REACHED THE ARTIFACT.** `ProcessContextSpec.co_created_shared` was asserted on the in-memory record only; `report._process_rule_json` dropped it, so the claim "a write into a shared project-level collection is not silent" was true of the object and FALSE of the artifact anyone reads, and had been since T076. It cost T107 its own evidence: BOTH the affix-process co-create and the phonological-rule path (`_copy_context_cell` into `tgt_phon_data.ContextsOS`) write that collection, so the destination's one `PhPhonData`-owned boundary context cannot be attributed to either. The serializer is FIXED (emitted unconditionally, empty list included) and pinned through the real function; the snapshot records `shared_context_attribution: "NOT DETERMINABLE FROM THIS RUN..."` rather than leaving a silence to read as success. **Filed as T108.** **VERDICT UNCHANGED AND NOT LAUNDERED**: both censuses `DUPLICATE_IDENTITY` / exit 3 on the same 3 `PhNCFeatures` duplicate extras -- T082's remaining `038-NK-P3`, untouched here. **THREE TRIPWIRES FIRED AND WERE EDITED ON THE EVIDENCE, NOT RELAXED**: T078's "still held behind the unexercised gate" test is INVERTED rather than deleted (and now also asserts `PhIterationContext` stayed put); T078's digest-reproducibility test is now a per-side table -- ejagham and mbugwe DESTINATIONS are `drifted` because these runs re-transferred them, all three SOURCES `match`, and T107's own artifacts reproduce on both sides, which is T102's chain one link further on; and T101's null-row pin went 12 -> 14 with clause 1 passing untouched (each new artifact nulls exactly `MoForm`/`MoMorphSynAnalysis`, `uncorroborated_null_rows` empty on both) -- and this is the FIRST such pair where the reading is NOT "reproduces its predecessor row for row", since the Ejagham artifact moves five rows on purpose, so its five moved rows are pinned BY NUMBER instead. **OPERATIONAL NOTE**: `debug/run038_before_after_pairs.py` calls `census_cli.main()` in the SAME process as the transfer, and the census half died on `SharedXMLBackendProvider.ShutdownInternal` -> `NullReferenceException` (exit 7, "no census artifact produced") while the transfer succeeded -- LCM's shared-XML backend does not survive a read-only re-open in a process that already opened the project for writing. - [X] **T096** [US1] **The code left a `TODO(contract)` waiting for a token; the contract delivered it 48 commits earlier and nothing on the branch could tell.** Surfaced 2026-08-21 by a REBASE -- not a wave and not a live run -- when `test_object_census.py::TestReasonVocabulary::test_tokens_match_the_schema_enum_exactly` failed on an enum length: 17 members in `census-artifact.schema.json`, 16 in `EXPECTED_REASON_TOKENS`, the extra one `SOURCE_REFERENT_ABSENT`. That tripwire was written to fire when the contract closed the gap and it did exactly that; it was not relaxed. The contract is `b2cb356` on `main` (2026-08-20), which appended the token to BOTH places the closed vocabulary lives (the 7.1 table in `fidelity-census.md` and `$defs.reasonToken.enum`), never reordered, and deliberately did NOT bump `schema_version` -- the EVOLUTION RULE's bump clause governs a SHIPPED version, this format has not shipped, and a bump would contradict the `CENSUS_SCHEMA_VERSION == 1` pin at `test_object_census.py:687` (precedent: `journal/T016-T020-census-engine.md:263-267`). **WHAT THE SUBSTITUTE ACTUALLY COST, which the TODO did not say.** While `census_cli.SOURCE_REFERENT_ABSENT_TOKEN` held `"DEPENDENCY_UNRESOLVED"`, the two rows of `DROP_REASON_TOKENS` that classify a missing referent yielded THE SAME TOKEN -- so `dropped_by_class_from_report` collapsed source-side and destination-side losses into ONE accounting line per class, and `accounted_for_drops`'s `if token == SOURCE_REFERENT_ABSENT_TOKEN` detail test matched every destination-side line too and stamped it `PROVISIONAL TOKEN: the referent was absent ON THE SOURCE`, which was false for them. READ from the committed `census-038-t091-ngoreme.json` (GT-20260821-184426): 4 classes, **1890 accounted items**, every one carrying that detail, and the artifact has no way left to say which side each belonged to. A least-wrong token is not a smaller version of the right one; it is a collision. **Closed the same pass** (`da5975f`): the token appended at the ONE canonical declaration, `Lib/models.py CENSUS_REASON_TOKENS` -- `Lib/census.py:20` states the rule in its own words, "THE VOCABULARIES ARE RE-EXPORTS, NEVER RE-DECLARATIONS" -- with no second declaration created and no reorder. `SOURCE_REFERENT_ABSENT_DETAIL` was **rewritten, not deleted**: its whole text explained a substitution that no longer occurs, so leaving it verbatim would put a false statement into every artifact line the token produces (the same defect one layer down), while deleting it would lose the only place a reviewer holding ONLY the artifact learns which side of the transfer the referent was missing from. **THE UNCOMFORTABLE FINDING, reported rather than smoothed over: the token is now correct and has no live producer.** MEASURED read-only across four corpora, `MoInflAffMsa` and `MoDerivAffMsa` -- the only subclasses where a null required POS is a DROP -- hold **0** null POSes: `Ngoreme FLEx` 0/134 and 0/3, `Ejagham W Mini` 0/111 and 0/0, `Mbugwe LizzieHC practice` 0/126 and 0/17, `Esperanto` 0/41 and 0/31. The drop that MOTIVATED the token was a `MoStemMsa` with an empty POS, and **T043b stopped dropping those** (null POS is legal there and is reproduced) -- its docstring names "2 of 164 `MoStemMsa` on the T038 pair, 9 on the Ngoreme pair", and the live probe finds 2 and 10, the same objects. So the contract closed a gap in the vocabulary while the engine closed the condition underneath it, and the two closures never met. Live run **GT-20260821-200656** (`Ngoreme FLEx` -> `GT038 Ngoreme After`, restored first; `Ngoreme Target`, `Ejagham W Target` and `Esperanto` untouched) stamps the token **0 times before and 0 after**, both MEASURED over the same run report; the artifact validates against `census-artifact.schema.json` with `schema_version` still 1. The emit path is therefore pinned in a test instead -- a full artifact carrying the token validates at version 1 -- because a vocabulary member nothing can emit is not a vocabulary member, it is a comment in an enum. That is T094's lesson arriving from the opposite direction, on a contract rather than a sweep. **Refused**: relaxing the exact-match tripwire, bumping `schema_version`, creating a second vocabulary declaration, re-pinning the unrelated pre-existing `Ngoreme FLEx` 1949 -> 1952 drift failure (left failing, noted), and fixing T090 (its canary held at 12 passed / 0 skipped either side of the live run). Meanwhile `compare_objects()` is **still the presence/absence stub**, so every run of this driver scores a four-valued taxonomy with a two-valued test. **Why this is a filing and not a fix**: the driver is a `debug/` instrument, not engine code, and replacing the stub changes what a full-copy sweep REPORTS -- `RESOLVED-BY-EQUIVALENCE` and `SILENTLY_UNSET` are precisely the two verdicts a presence/absence check cannot distinguish from `RESOLVED` and `DANGLING` respectively, so the corrected instrument will disagree with every sweep artifact previously committed. That needs its own before/after, not a drive-by. **Do not close this by deleting the TODO** - `debug/run_fullcopy_sweep.py` **CLOSED 2026-08-24 (commit `2c5d797`), AND THE FILING NEEDS TWO CORRECTIONS.** The premise had indeed expired and the taxonomy is indeed ratified -- but **the replacement was already written, on the branch that owns the file**. `035-fullsweep-fidelity` has no `compare_objects` at all: commits `66fe390` ("T031-T034 -- the object plane can now fail honestly") and `58cc970` ("T045a -- the measurements finally reach the guards") replaced it with `reconcile_project_objects` / `findings_from_accounting` / `payload_never_compared` / `drop_records_from_artifact` over a `debug/fullsweep/` package **that does not exist in this worktree**, and its own comment names this stub's TODO: *"Its TODO said the verdict taxonomy was still in review; the ratified spec settled it, and `fullsweep.compare.reconcile_objects` implements it (T031)."* This branch's copy has exactly ONE commit (`8c72bdc`, the original skeleton), so re-implementing here would fork another feature's instrument, duplicate finished work and conflict with the branch that is ahead. **CORRECTION 1**: the four-valued vocabulary is `link_findings[].classification`, a claim about a LINK; `findings[]` carries `kind` over class/category/field/source/target. This seam receives `{class: {guid}}` inventories -- no links, no fields -- so it could never have been upgraded to that vocabulary without a different INPUT. 035 did not upgrade the verdict; it replaced the question. **CORRECTION 2**: the stub's rows violate a SETTLED requirement, not an unratified one -- FR-145 fails any finding whose labels are "empty, placeholder, or identical regardless of subject", and every row it emits has `category=None, field=None, target_value=None` plus the subject-invariant token `NOT_YET_CLASSIFIED_MISSING_FROM_TARGET`. So the fix never depended on Groups E-P at all. The TODO is NOT deleted (it is still true of the code beneath it on this branch); it now names where the fix lives, so the next `PROVISIONAL`/`TODO(contract)` sweep does not file it a third time. No behaviour changed, so the before/after the task line requires -- which attaches to REPLACING the stub -- is not owed. Thirteenth appearance of the recurring shape and a new variety: read at the wrong REPOSITORY STATE rather than the wrong level, by an instrument that cannot see across branches. See journal/T097-the-fix-that-was-already-written.md

- [X] **T099** [US2] **The census emits a count it knows is false, and on a neighbouring path refuses to emit anything at all -- both because the in-memory model cannot say `null` that the schema already permits.** Raised 2026-08-21 by T096's sweep; **the most serious of the four**, because it is the acceptance instrument reporting a number it has itself flagged as untrue. `census_cli.py:126-136` states it outright at `:130`: *"OPEN ITEM, flagged rather than papered over: the schema's honest value here is null and the in-memory model cannot express it"*, and the value is emitted anyway at `:1591-1593` -- `MoForm` and `MoMorphSynAnalysis` rows carry a knowingly-false `0` / `0`. `census_cli.py:518-533` is the same root cause with the opposite failure mode: at `:523`, *"`models.ClassCensusRow` cannot carry a null count, so there is no honest row to write"* -- and it **aborts the whole run** rather than emitting the row the schema allows. **The contract side is already done, and it forbids exactly what the code does.** `contracts/census-artifact.schema.json:389-395` types `source_count` and `destination_count_total` as `["integer", "null"]`, and `source_count`'s `$comment` draws the distinction in as many words: *"null only on a NOT_EVALUATED row where the class could not be counted at all; **a genuine zero is 0, never null**"*. The contract therefore requires uncountable and genuinely-empty to be distinguishable, and the emitted `0` collapses them -- so a reader cannot tell "this class holds nothing" from "this class was never counted". **The sole blocker is in-repo and owes no spec decision**: `Lib/models.py:1134` declares `source_count: int`, with `:1148-1154` the surrounding validation. Widen the model to the schema's own type, then the false `0` becomes `null` and the abort becomes a row. **Why this is not folded into T096**: T096 corrected a token in the reason vocabulary, which is a labelling change; this changes emitted COUNTS in the artifact that every phase gate reads, so it needs its own census and a re-read of any predicate that compares a count against 0. **Do not close it by making the abort at `:518-533` silent** -- an abort is at least loud, and Principle I prefers a refusal to a fabrication. The `0`s are the half that violates it **CLOSED 2026-08-21** (`2fce617`), and the measurement is a pair of real census runs eight minutes apart with the fix as the only difference: `_snapshots/census-038-t099-ngoreme-before.json` and `-after.json` (`Ngoreme FLEx` -> `GT038 Ngoreme After`, same baseline, same run report, both projects asserted byte-identical across the pair by digest -- which matters because that source HAS drifted, and a diff against T096's committed artifact would have read 10 moved rows and `total_shortfall` 70638 -> 70659 as if the fix had done it). **THE DELTA: exactly TWO rows, and no gate quantity at all.** `MoForm` and `MoMorphSynAnalysis` each move five fields from `0` to `null` (`source_count`, `destination_count_total`, `destination_count_net`, `difference`, `difference_raw`); `total_shortfall` 70659, `unexplained_shortfall` 68941, `classes_not_evaluated` 3, `classes_matched` 41 and verdict DUPLICATE_IDENTITY / exit 3 are byte-identical either side, because `build_totals` filters `difference is not None` before summing. Both artifacts validate against the schema with 0 section-11 invariant failures. **THREE PLACES THE `int` WAS LOAD-BEARING, none visible from the field declaration and none predicted by the filing.** (1) `census.class_row_artifact` dropped any mapped field whose value was None, on a comment asserting no REQUIRED field could be None -- true only *because* the model lied, so the honest fix would have omitted three `$defs.classRow.required` keys and produced an INVALID artifact; the guard is now on the field name (`models.CLASS_ROW_REQUIRED_NULLABLE_FIELDS`, derived in the test from the schema's own `required` array). (2) `verdict_class` compared `difference == 0` BEFORE checking None -- the exact line where a placeholder zero became the word MATCHED. (3) `counts_pass` did the same, where `None == 0` is False, so an uncounted gate-relevant row would have FAILED on counts and reported a loss nobody measured. **THE ABORT BECAME A ROW AND STAYED LOUD**: `_print_unmeasurable` keeps every `[FAIL]` line and the "may not fail QUIETLY" summary and now RETURNS the entries, which become `errors[]` -- CENSUS_ERROR / exit 7 in `recompute_verdict`, which the gate recomputes rather than reads -- so the run fails at the same code and finally writes the document naming the class, the project and the reason. `capture_baseline` KEEPS its abort deliberately: a baseline is a count map a later run SUBTRACTS, a null there would be subtracted as 0 by exactly the arithmetic 5.2 forbids, and a baseline document has no `classes` array for a row to go into. **THE UNCOMFORTABLE FINDING, and a test found it rather than inspection: the fix opens a laundering path.** A null difference reads NOT_EVALUATED and `row_passes` returns True for NOT_EVALUATED, so nulling a required class retires its shortfall without measuring anything -- unreachable before only because the model refused to hold a null. Closed on the PRODUCER (`_refuse_uncorroborated_nulls`: a required row may carry a null only if it is advisory or named in `errors[]`), NOT on the gate, and the gate half is filed as **T101**. **THE PREDICATE RE-READ** found the library already null-ready in about a dozen places -- `net_destination_count`, `signed_difference`, `unexplained_counts`, `row_verdict_class`, `MatchBasis.sums_to`, invariants 3/4/11, `build_totals`, `row_passes`, and `census_cli diff`'s `isinstance(old, int)` guard whose message already read "a null destination_count_total on one side" -- ALL OF IT DEAD, because a `ClassCensusRow` could not produce the input. This fix added no null handling; it made existing null handling reachable. That is the feature's recurring shape once more, and the first time the thing read at the wrong level is the HANDLING itself, unreachable from the type of a dataclass field. `_phase_5` is the one predicate that `continue`s on NOT_EVALUATED without failing; `_require_matched` (P1-P4) FAILS on it, the safe direction, now pinned. `census_cli diff` moves the two rows from "compared and equal" to "not compared" (`[INFO]`, no verdict change) -- a real, small coverage loss and the right one, since comparing two falsehoods and finding them equal was never evidence. **Also filed: T100** -- the closed 17-token vocabulary has no member meaning "the accessor did not resolve", so an unresolved-accessor row cannot carry the `not_evaluated_reason` the schema's `$comment` asks for; not settled by inventing an 18th token. **Pins deliberately moved**, both wording and neither a relaxation: `_NOT_MEASURED_NOTE` no longer calls its own counts placeholders (leaving it verbatim would put a false statement into every line it produces -- T096's `SOURCE_REFERENT_ABSENT_DETAIL` lesson), and `_report_unmeasurable` split into a reporting half and a raising half (no test asserted on it before; the `capture_baseline` raise is pinned now and was not). `tests/unit` 3534 -> 3558 passed (79 skipped, 14 xfailed); `tests/integration` 409 -> 427 passed, 76 skipped, **0 failed -- and that is NOT a win**: T096's one pre-existing failure (`test_ngoreme_flex_holds_1949_and_ngoreme_holds_1945`, live drift to 1952) became a SKIP because FieldWorks was opened on `Ngoreme FLEx` at 23:43 (PID 13768, verified running -- a genuine lock, not T090's stale one) and the census correctly refuses a locked project. The failure did not go away, it went out of reach; the lock was left alone. That same lock is why the committed after-artifact predates `_refuse_uncorroborated_nulls`, so the guard is run over the committed rows instead (`test_the_committed_measurement_satisfies_the_producer_guard`). **Refused**: silencing or downgrading the abort, removing the `capture_baseline` abort because it shared a function, inventing an 18th reason token, adding T101's gate invariant as a drive-by, re-pinning the 1949 drift, clearing a live FieldWorks lock for a cleaner-looking suite, and re-running the census to regenerate the after-artifact once the tree was dirty.
