## Phase 7: US3 - Selecting a piece brings what it needs (P3)

**Goal**: `Lib/closure.py` stops being code with no caller, and a selected affix actually pulls the categories, slots and templates it needs.

**Independent Test**: select only affixes, run a preview, and confirm the dependent categories, slots and templates appear in the plan and are individually deselectable.

**Depends on Phase 2's registry.** Holds the `categories.py` claim for its duration. Defect G2 verified: `closure.py`'s only importer today is `tests/unit/test_closure.py:12`.

**Audit-then-enable, one relationship at a time, with a census run after each (R3).** A global flag cannot satisfy FR-018. 037's rewritten `natural_classes_dependencies` is deliberately **not** among the first three.

### Tests

- [X] **T065** [US3] Write failing tests that an edge may influence a plan only after it is registered in `CLOSURE_EDGES_VERIFIED`, and that `build_run_plan` raises on `verified is False` (FR-018) - `tests/unit/test_038_closure.py` **DONE** (`fd09594`). **Not failing-then-passing, because the code was already there** -- the registry, `closure_dependencies_for` and the preview-side gate all landed with the Phase 2 foundational work, so these tests' value is entirely in whether they catch a regression, and that is what was measured rather than asserted. `test_038_foundational.py` already has a class NAMED `TestBuildRunPlanClosureGate` whose docstring says "`build_run_plan` must RAISE on an unverified edge" -- but every assertion in it calls the helper `_materialise_closure_edges` directly and `build_run_plan` is never invoked. **The fourth appearance of the T048b/T086/T087 shape**: a signal that genuinely exists, read at a level where it cannot do its job. Proved: wrapping the call site in `except Exception: _closure_edges = ()` leaves **all 60 foundational tests passing** while 5 of these fail. Covers the trichotomy one global flag cannot express -- unregistered -> producer never consulted; registered-but-unverified -> `build_run_plan` raises; registered-and-verified -> the edge reaches `plan.closure_edges` as `origin="pulled_in"`. The third group is load-bearing: without it the first two are both satisfiable by a closure walk that is simply broken. **One of my own tests was wrong and a mutation caught it**: the first `test_an_unregistered_producer_is_never_called` passed for a weaker reason than it claimed, because with an EMPTY registry `_walk_verified_closure` short-circuits before the per-relationship gate is ever reached -- a fall-through mutation to `LEAF_CATEGORIES[...]["dependencies"]` slipped through all 10 tests. Replaced with `test_a_registered_edge_does_not_admit_its_unregistered_neighbour`, which runs the walk with AFFIX_TO_POS verified and SLOTS registered nowhere; that version catches it twice. 11 tests, 3 mutation directions verified. `tests/unit` 3422 passed. See journal/T065-T067-the-audit-that-said-no.md

Three decisions the numbers are made of. (1) **A record is emitted only when the DEPENDENT is actually being written** -- that is why 53 edges are 35 records: the 18 `SLOT_TO_POS` edges name slots that were themselves refused, so they do not arrive and are already dropped-with-reason via their own `DEPENDENCY_DESELECTED` skip. The count was 1949 when the test was written, 1952 at T087, 1953 now. **NOT caused by T100/T101**: the failure reproduces on the stashed pre-change tree, and the suite arithmetic agrees from the other side -- skipped went 76 -> 75 while failed went 0 -> 1, so this is a live test that was SKIPPED during the T087 pass and now runs. Three directions, and they are not equivalent: (a) re-pin to 1953, which buys green until the next edit and is what the last two passes declined to do; (b) assert the PREMISE rather than the number -- `Ngoreme FLEx` holds MORE `MoStemMsa` than `Ngoreme`, and both hold the phoneme counts the corrected premise turns on -- which is what the test's own name says it is for; or (c) gate the exact-count assertion on the digest, the way `_t024_census` already makes SNAPSHOT-based blocks stand down on drift, and report rather than fail when the project has moved. The test's own comment argues AGAINST (c) in its current form (*"a moved file makes it MORE useful, not less"*) -- that reasoning is sound for read-only-ness, which the before/after digest comparison proves independently, and is what makes (b) the likely answer. **Do not close this by deleting the test or by marking it xfail**: it is the only live proof that the corrected premise holds at all, and a premise nobody measures is the shape this feature keeps filing - `tests/integration/test_object_census.py` **CLOSED 2026-08-22** (`f0801a9`), by **(b) with a BOUNDED (c)**, because the two claims in that test are not the same kind of claim. **THE PREMISE IS NOW ASSERTED UNCONDITIONALLY** against whatever is on disk -- the two projects differ, both count nonzero (a 0-vs-0 vacuous pass is refused), and `Ngoreme FLEx` holds MORE `MoStemMsa` AND more `PhPhoneme` -- which honours the test comment's *"a moved file makes it MORE useful, not less"*, sound about the premise and only about the premise. **THE EXACT COUNTS move into an append-only `sha256(.fwdata) -> (MoStemMsa, PhPhoneme)` ledger** and are asserted ONLY against the bytes they were measured from: at UNCHANGED bytes an exact count is a real tripwire (the same file must count the same way), at CHANGED bytes it is a claim about a file that no longer exists, so it stands down and prints the exact line to append -- the same stand-down `_t024_census` already applies to snapshot-based blocks, now scoped to the one claim that is literally "these bytes counted N". Both readings recorded: `052243ea...` -> (1949, 41), the digest the committed snapshot was measured at, and `e10a44ef...` -> **(1953, 41)**, measured today. **1952 gets NO row** -- it was seen in a failure message with no digest captured, and a count without the bytes it was counted from is exactly what the ledger exists to stop. `Ngoreme` measures **(1945, 37)** at `6d35c957...`, unchanged. **A GATE THAT CAN STAND DOWN CAN BE TALKED INTO STANDING DOWN**, so two guards: an OFFLINE test (runs with no FieldWorks present) asserts every ledger row satisfies the premise, so a contradicting row cannot be appended to buy green and the block cannot become a no-op by the ledger being emptied; and a run where BOTH projects drift off the ledger FAILS, because a run that checked no exact count anywhere must not read as green. **PROVED BY FORGING, THREE WAYS**: row set to (9999, 41) at the unchanged digest -> FAILS naming the same-bytes contradiction; digest key corrupted so the project drifts off -> PASSES, premise asserted, prints the line to append; `Ngoreme`'s row set to (9999, 99) -> offline test FAILS at `assert 1949 > 9999`. **Depends on Phase 7.** Until now these 6 rules were reported and skipped by T055.

- [x] **T076** [US5] Extend closure to pull the `PhSimpleContext*` objects owned by `PhPhonData.ContextsOS` that a rule's `PhSequenceContext.MembersRS` references, register that edge under `CLOSURE_EDGES_VERIFIED`, and flip the 6 affected rules from report-and-skip to full transfer - `src/gramtrans/Lib/categories.py` **DONE 2026-08-22, and this line's FIRST CLAUSE IS NOT WHAT SHIPPED -- the audit refused it.** **MEASURED READ-ONLY FIRST** (`debug/audit038_t076_process_contexts.py`, artifact `_snapshots/t076-process-context-audit.json`, both corpora): the 6 condition-4 far endpoints on `Mbugwe LizzieHC practice` (5 `PhSimpleContextNC`, 1 `PhSimpleContextSeg`) are **6 of 6 owned by `PhPhonData.ContextsOS`** (owner `PhPhonData`, flid 5099004) and **0 of 6 reachable from `PhPhonData.PhonRulesOS`** -- `_copy_context_cell` is the only path in this engine that creates a `PhSimpleContext*` into a destination's `ContextsOS` and it never touches these, and **no `GrammarCategory` enumerates a member of `ContextsOS` at all**. **So "extend closure to pull them" names the exact far endpoint T089 REFUSED**: FR-015 needs a pulled-in item visible in the plan and FR-016 needs it individually deselectable, and an item no `enumerate_source` yields has no `PlannedAction`, hence no row to show and no checkbox to clear. Registering it would have repeated T089's defect with a `verified_by` on it. Fourteenth appearance of this feature's recurring shape and the first where the thing read at the wrong level is THE INSTRUCTION. **WHAT SHIPPED: the context is CO-CREATED and the RESOLVABILITY TEST MOVED ONE HOP OUT.** `_PROCESS_SHARED_CONTEXT_CLASSES` adds no create code -- a shared context is structurally the SAME object as one of the rule's own `InputOS` members and differs only in its owner -- so the executor builds it into the destination's `ContextsOS`, GUID-preserving, the co-creation contract `inflection_features_execute_action` already uses for its symbolic values. The old check asked "is this context in the destination", where `None` only ever meant *nobody has copied this yet*; the new one asks whether its `FeatureStructureRA` referent resolves -- the thing this engine genuinely cannot invent -- and a context whose phoneme is missing STILL skips, because co-creating it would produce a context matching nothing while the run reported success. **Identity is still tried FIRST**, which is what makes it idempotent under SC-008 (mutation M5 proves that ordering is load-bearing). **WHAT IS REGISTERED is the referent edge one hop out**, measured **6 of 6 enumerable**: `PROCESS_RULE_TO_PHONEME` (32 edges / 16 distinct, foreign 0, unresolved 0, owned-value 0) and `PROCESS_RULE_TO_NATURAL_CLASS` (10 / 2, same zeros), both walking the rule's OWN `InputOS` contexts AND the shared ones its sequences reference. **THESE ARE THE FIRST TWO REGISTRY ROWS ON SINGLE-CORPUS EVIDENCE and both say so in their own `verified_by`**: `Ejagham Mini` holds ZERO `MoAffixProcess` rules, so it reads `NO_DATA`, which the driver distinguishes from `CONFIRMED` precisely so a corpus holding nothing cannot rubber-stamp an edge. That forced `test_only_the_confirmed_relationships_are_registered`'s rule ("CONFIRMED on every corpus") to change, and the replacement is NOT a relaxation: REFUSED on no corpus, CONFIRMED on at least a per-row floor (2 where both corpora have data, 1 for these two), and every non-CONFIRMED reading must be `NO_DATA` **and nothing else** -- so fewer confirmations can only mean "the corpus held none", never "a corpus disagreed". **A SECOND EXPIRED PREMISE, found by running the registration census.** `run038_closure_census.py` failed with "AFFIXES-only plan composition CHANGED", on its own docstring's premise that *"T070 ... and T072 ... have not landed, so a registration must add EDGES and change no decision"*. They HAVE landed, and planning the pulled-in items IS T070 -- a driver still demanding no change would refuse every future registration for working. Replaced with a STRICTER claim: every added action or overwrite is accounted for by a pulled-in reference **one for one** -- measured 26 = 26 (phonemes 5+11, POS 4+2, NC 2+0, types 2+0 against pulled-in 16/6/2/2), `enrichments` +2 as the UPDATE leg, every other counter required unmoved. The FULL-COPY claim is untouched and stays unconditional (0 edges live and empty, composition byte-identical). **MUTATION-VERIFIED EIGHT WAYS WITH ONE HONEST NEGATIVE THAT BECAME PART OF THE FIX.** M1 (shared classes emptied) 5 FAIL; M3 (referent never checked) 1; M4 (co-creation unreported) 1; M5 (identity leg dropped) 1; M6 (producer skips shared contexts) 1; M7 (constraint guard dropped) 1; M2b (owner predicate -> True) 2. **M2 (owner COMPARISON -> True) SURVIVED**: the only fake reaching that check had no `Owner` and was refused a line earlier, so the half of the predicate saying WHICH owner counts was untested and a context owned by anything else would have quietly acquired an unmeasured create path. Closed with `test_a_context_owned_by_anything_but_phon_data_is_not_co_created`. **REPORTING**: `ProcessContextSpec.co_created_shared` is additive, defaults empty, and names the source GUIDs this run BUILT rather than found -- SC-010 admits no unreported outcome, and a write into a shared project-level collection made as a side effect of transferring a lexical entry is the write a reader is least able to see. Both directions tested; 12 of 18 rules co-create nothing. **DELIBERATELY NOT ADMITTED**: `PhSimpleContextBdry` and `PhIterationContext` stay behind `_PROCESS_UNEXERCISED_CLASSES`. Mbugwe really holds 22 and 11 of them in `ContextsOS` and NOT ONE is referenced by any of the 18 rules, so admitting them would ship a create path no corpus can check. Pinned by a parametrised test. Three registry tripwires fired and each was edited deliberately with its evidence, never relaxed. **Nothing filed.** See journal/T076-T077-the-dependency-nothing-could-enumerate.md

**Added 2026-08-26 by user decision at the resume gate.** T081 measured that P5

code", and named a residue that "no task in this feature owns". The decision is
to **own it here** rather than file it out to a successor: this phase is what
makes P5 turnable.

than over a re-measurement would have shipped with seven rows still red, so the
list below is the measurement.

**`MoStemMsa -1` ON NGOREME IS THE ROW TO LOOK AT FIRST, because it is a PHASE 1
class.** P1 requires it MATCHED and T038 records the predicate satisfied - on
`CENSUS-20260820-114752`, a different artifact from the T078 trio. One of those
two readings is stale and this phase must find out which before anything is
built on top of the other fifteen rows. Bounded: it is a single object.

**Every W1 task is a READ-ONLY ATTRIBUTION PROBE and is deliberately separated
from its fix.** This feature's own record is that the expensive mistake is
building a create path before knowing why the objects are missing - T107 gave
`PhSimpleContextBdry` a create path on the affix-process route and the class
stayed red on two pairs because the loss was on the phonological-rule route
instead. A probe answers "which code path drops this, and on which route", and
its answer FIXES THE SCOPE of the W2 task that follows it; a W2 task line that
still reads as written here has not had its probe yet.

**The sanctioned corpus problem is real and does NOT block W1.** T081 records
that the T078 trio "is not reproducible as measured" - the sources have moved
off their pinned digests and `Ngoreme Target` must never be restored. That
bounds re-CENSUS, not attribution: why a class fails to transfer is a property
of the code path, readable against any sanctioned pair (`Ejagham Mini`,
`Mbugwe LizzieHC practice`) into a fresh throwaway target. W3's re-gate is the
task that inherits the corpus problem, and it says so.

## Dependencies & Execution Order

**Phase dependencies**

```text
Phase 1 Setup
   -> Phase 2 Foundational          (blocks every story)
        -> Phase 3 US2 census       (the acceptance instrument for everything below)
             -> Phase 4 US1 natural key   [external gate: T028, 035's roster append]
                  -> Phase 5 US4 enrichment  ||  Phase 6 US5a process rules
                       -> Phase 7 US3 closure
                            -> Phase 8 US5b shared contexts
                                 -> Phase 9 Polish  [external gate: T078, 037 landing]
                                      -> Phase 10 US6 P5 residue  [T081's gate]
```

Phase 10 was added 2026-08-26 by user decision at the resume gate. T081 measured
P5 as blocked "on a scoping decision this feature has not taken, not on
unfinished code"; the decision is to take the residue INTO 038, so Phase 10 is
what makes P5 turnable and **T081 and T085 both now wait on it**.

Phases 5 and 6 are genuinely parallel except for `models.py` (T042 and T052) and the
`categories.py` claim, which must be serialised between them.

**Wave restatement per phase**

- **Phase 1**: W1 T001/T002/T003 [P] -> W2 T004 (claims) -> W3 T005 (rebase) -> W4 T006 (green baseline).
- **Phase 2**: W1 T007 -> W2 T008 (same file) -> W3 T009/T010/T011 [P] -> W4 T012 (needs T008 + T010) -> W5 T013.
- **Phase 3**: T014 (failing test) -> W1 T015 -> W2..W6 T016-T020 (all `census.py`, serial) -> W7 T021/T022 [P] -> W8 T023, T024, T025.
- **Phase 4**: T026/T027 (failing tests) -> W1 T028 (**hard external gate**) -> W2 T029/T030 [P] -> W3 T031 (plan-time first) -> W4 T032-T035 (`categories.py`, serial) -> W5 T036/T037 [P] -> W6 T038, T039.
- **Phase 5**: T040/T041 -> W1 T042 -> W2 T043, T044 (`categories.py`, serial) -> W3 T045/T046/T047 [P] -> W4 T048.
- **Phase 6**: T049/T050/T051 -> W1 T052 -> W2 T053-T058 (`categories.py`, serial) -> W3 T059/T060/T061/T062 [P] -> W4 T063, T064.
- **Phase 7**: T065 -> W1 T066 -> W2 T067, T068, T069 (one edge at a time, census after each) -> W3 T070/T071/T072 [P] -> W4 T073, T074 -> W5 T075. **Amended 2026-08-21 by T067's audit.** T065 and T066 are closed (T066 by prior work). W2 does NOT run as written: **T088 now precedes T067**, because the audit T067 requires found `affixes_dependencies` dead on live data, and an edge whose producer reads nothing cannot earn a `verified_by`. T068 and T069 audited CLEAN and are not blocked by T067 -- a per-relationship allowlist exists exactly so one relationship can go live while another cannot -- so the runnable W2 order was **T068, T069, T088, T067**. **T088 landed 2026-08-21**, and **T067 closed the same day** with the member split done: two of its three relationships registered (`AFFIX_TO_POS`, `MSA_TO_FEAT_STRUC_TYPE`) and the third (`MSA_TO_INFL_FEATURE`) refused for a far endpoint no category can enumerate -- filed as **T089**. So the runnable W2 order was **T068, T069, T088, T067**, and T068/T069 still owe registration plus a census each; T067's narrow-producer pattern (explicit `dependency_category` per row, one far category per producer) is what they should copy, and its census driver (`debug/run038_t067_census.py`) is reusable -- note especially that a FULL COPY cannot observe a closure edge at all, so the acceptance selection has to be the narrow one. T075 (P2's gate) is already satisfied without any closure at all, which is worth remembering before registering anything: SC-003/SC-004 may be closable by T069 alone. **W2 CLOSED 2026-08-21.** `T068` (`072065b`) and `T069` (`4fc8e82`) both registered, one census each, in that order. Five rows now stand over three source categories -- `AFFIX_TO_POS`, `MSA_TO_FEAT_STRUC_TYPE`, `SLOT_TO_POS`, `TEMPLATE_TO_POS`, `TEMPLATE_TO_SLOT` -- with `MSA_TO_INFL_FEATURE` still refused (T089) and `SLOT_TO_TEMPLATE` / `AFFIX_TO_SLOT` unregistrable because nothing emits them. **Two of the plan's seven member names were wrong in the same region**: the slot/template arrow runs template->slot, not slot->template, so `SLOT_TO_POS` and `TEMPLATE_TO_SLOT` had to be added and `SLOT_TO_TEMPLATE` is emitted by nothing. **And the suspicion above is settled**: SC-004 needed nothing from Phase 7 (P2 was already green), and **SC-003 is NOT closable by T069** -- it is FR-019, its arrow is `AFFIX_TO_SLOT` via `RunPlan.msa_slot_bindings`, and it belongs to T074. W3 (T070/T071/T072) is now unblocked, and it inherits a measured **two-hop** closure (templates -> slots -> POSes, 53 edges over 23 distinct pulled-in refs under an AFFIX_TEMPLATES-only selection), so FR-015 marking and FR-016 deselection have to handle a pulled-in item whose puller was itself pulled in. **W3 CLOSED 2026-08-21.** All three landed together because they are one mechanism seen from three files: T070 plans the pulled-in members (`preview._plan_pulled_in_items`), T071 lets the user refuse them through the two knobs that already existed and adds no machinery, T072 gives the wizard a way to put a GUID in `excluded_deps` at all -- it never could. The two-hop case this note warned about is handled by construction: the walk already produced the transitive edges, and the pull-in keys on the DEPENDENCY ref, so a slot pulled in by a template and a POS pulled in by that slot are both planned, each naming its own puller. **The measurement is the headline**: a templates-only transfer used to land 8 of 11 templates and 0 of 19 slots, silently. **W4 (T073, T074) is now unblocked**, and T073 inherits a written input it did not have before -- `ClosureEdge.deselected` is now set (53 of 53 under a full deselection), which is what its `IncompletenessRecord`s read. **T073 CLOSED 2026-08-21** (`4cebfb2`), and it read exactly that input: 53 deselected edges become **35** `IncompletenessRecord`s over 11 distinct arriving templates, 0 when nothing is deselected, 29 when only the 5 POSes are (the two-hop case, where the 18 slots arrive and arrive incomplete), 0 on a full copy. **No `models.py` change was needed** -- all six surfaces existed and were fed an empty tuple, so Phase 5/6's serialisation claim on that file was never touched. Three things W4's remaining task should inherit. (1) **The accounting rule**: a record is emitted only when the DEPENDENT is actually being written, because an item that does not arrive is dropped-with-reason and SC-010 admits exactly one bucket per item -- that rule, not a shortfall, is why 53 edges are 35 records. (2) **Satisfaction is read off the PLAN, not the skip list**, which is what catches `_plan_pulled_in_items`'s silent `result is None` exit; T074's FR-019 link failures emit an `IncompletenessRecord` too (data-model.md), so it should reuse `_plan_incompleteness`'s vocabulary rather than open-code a second one. (3) **`closure.topological` detects a dependency cycle and discards the finding**; T073 recovers it from the edges (`_closure_cycle_groups`) rather than change that contract, and the live cycle count is 0 because the five registered relationships are a diamond. **One new task filed: T093** -- 8 of the 35 records name a dependency that is already in the destination, where nothing is incomplete; pinned as current behaviour, and unfixable inside T073 because T071 correctly suppresses a refused ref before its planner runs. **W4 now reduces to T074 alone** (FR-019 / SC-003, baseline 0 of 110 linked and 0 reported). **W2's refusal was LIFTED 2026-08-22 by T089, and the row is still not registered -- read both halves.** `MSA_TO_INFL_FEATURE`'s far endpoints were `IFsSymFeatVal` symbolic values because `_feat_struc_deps` emitted the VALUE named by a `FeatureSpecsOC` entry's `ValueRA`; it now emits the feature that OWNS that value, and the audit returns **CONFIRMED on both corpora** (99 edges over 4 distinct far GUIDs / 17 over 2, `resolved_as_owned_value` 0). Note the direction: the edge set got SMALLER, 34 far GUIDs collapsing onto the 4 that already existed. The registration is **T104** and is deliberately a separate task -- T089's census varies the PRODUCER with the registry held fixed (and measured no plan change under either selection, with the census reproducing `census-038-t076-registered.json` row for row), while a registration needs the other axis, `run038_closure_census.py` varying the registry with the producer held fixed. A driver that answers one of those questions cannot answer the other. **T104 LANDED 2026-08-22** (`2358eb5`) and ran exactly that other axis: AFFIXES-only, 358 closure edges over 30 distinct pulled-in refs (the row's own share 99 over 4), 0 with the registry emptied, 0 under a full copy, and the one-for-one accounting closing at 30 = 30 per category. The delta against T076's run under the same selection is +99 edges and +4 pulled-in refs with every prior kind byte-identical -- the registration added its own row and nothing else. Five registry tripwires fired and each was edited deliberately; one of them (`test_the_inflection_feature_half_is_still_not_registered`) had to be INVERTED, and its replacement is stricter than the assertion it replaced. **Phase 7's registry now stands at eight rows over four source categories.**
- **Phase 8**: T076 -> T077.
- **Phase 9**: W1 T078/T079 [P] -> W2 T080/T081/T082 + T109/T110 [P] -> W3 T083, T084, T085. **T081 and T085 are now gated on Phase 10** (T081 on T125, T085 on both).

The `MoStemMsa -1` row inside T118 is checked FIRST: it is a Phase 1 class, so one of P1's two readings is stale and it costs one object to find out which.
- **Out of band**: T086 (`Lib/census.py`, the phase gate's own scope). It belongs to no wave because it is the instrument, not a story: T038, T075 and T048 could not close without it, and each of them sits in an EARLIER phase than the T079 accounting their exit-0 clause was waiting on. - **Out of band**: T087 (`Lib/census.py`, reported-but-unreadable rule losses) and T088 (`Lib/categories.py`, uncast reads of subclass-only properties). Both were raised by live runs rather than by a wave, and both are the same recurring shape this feature keeps meeting -- something that exists but is read at a level where it cannot do its job. T088 was the widest: a producer-level defect class rather than one site, and the reason Phase 7's W2 order had to be amended. Filed and CLOSED 2026-08-21 (`955267e`) -- narrower on inspection than first reported (four sites, all reads of polymorphic owning-collection members; 037's producer was a false positive and casts correctly). T087 remains open. **T089** joined them 2026-08-21, filed by T067's per-relationship audit: `MSA_TO_INFL_FEATURE`'s far endpoints are mostly `IFsSymFeatVal` symbolic values that no category enumerates, so the relationship is live, narrow and still unregistrable. Same recurring shape once more -- something that exists and is read at a level where it cannot do its job -- this time on the far side of the edge rather than the near side. **T090** joined them 2026-08-21, filed by T069's test runs: the Phase 7 drivers leave a stale `<project>.fwdata.lock` behind, and `test_038_phon_empty_drop_live.py` reads its presence as a live FieldWorks lock and skips -- so running a driver makes the next suite run 2 skips quieter (77 -> 75 after clearing them) in a way that reads as green. Same shape again, on the harness rather than the engine. Also recorded under T069 rather than filed, because it was FIXED there: the only unit coverage of `slots_dependencies` and `affix_templates_dependencies` -- the two producers T068/T069 registered -- sat behind **stale non-strict `xfail` marks**, 14 tests all XPASSING and therefore unable to fail, which is why two mutations that break those producers outright left `tests/unit` green. **T091** and **T092** joined them 2026-08-21, both from the same trace -- the first same-pair before/after run of the two corpora the feature's defect table was measured on, and the diagnosis it triggered. **T091 is RC-1 itself**, the defect this feature's highest-value phase exists to close, found still live on the pair that motivated it: `PartOfSpeech` is on the roster and the fallback never reaches the POS plan path, so a full copy of `Ngoreme FLEx` creates a second `Verb`, `Noun`, `Pronoun`, `Adverb` and `Pro-form` beside the starter ones. It is out of band because Phase 4 closed on Ejagham, and Ejagham CANNOT see it -- its starters carry the GOLD GUIDs and match on identity, so Ngoreme is the only pair in the corpus that exercises the fallback at all. That is the corpus lesson of this feature stated most sharply: a phase gate is only as good as the pair it ran on. Diagnosis swept the shape and found **two** sites, not one (`PartOfSpeech` live, `LexEntryInflType` latent), which is T088's producer-level lesson recurring at a smaller count. **T092** is the same trace's by-product and the eighth instance of the recurring shape: `preview.plan_match_decision`, T031's plan-time seam, has zero production callers -- Defect G2's exact shape (correct machinery, only its own unit test importing it) on the seam that was supposed to prevent T091's divergence. Both remain open; T091 lands first, and T092's routing decision is made on the census T091 produces rather than folded into it. **T091 LANDED 2026-08-21 and did NOT close** (`223f033` on `038-t091-pos-natural-key`), which is the most instructive out-of-band result the feature has produced. The diagnosed insertion is correct and the row predicate is green -- `census.row_passes` on Ngoreme's `PartOfSpeech` is True, its five duplicate groups are gone, `starter_matched_to_source` 0 -> 5, `identity_substitution.per_category["GRAM_CATEGORIES"]` 5, and `Ejagham W Mini` is byte-identical. Two things the diagnosis did not predict came out of the live runs. First, "the executor needs nothing" was wrong: the POS CREATE path resolved a sub-POS's parent by source GUID and dropped it silently, so run 1 read `PartOfSpeech 26 -> 14` and lost 12 descendants of the five remapped categories -- and run 2 still lost them, because a `IPartOfSpeech(src_owner)` cast was being computed as an exception probe and THROWN AWAY, which is T088's defect class verbatim on a line whose own comment knew it needed the cast. Second, and the reason T091 stays open: **the duplicate was load-bearing.** 1,848 MSAs were resolving their part of speech THROUGH the duplicate `Noun` and `Verb`, and the five remaining two-positional `_resolve_target_pos` call sites cannot follow the substitution, so removing 5 duplicate objects costs 1,848 senses their morphosyntactic analysis. Filed as **T094**, with the sites named. The census stayed honest throughout -- `unexplained_shortfall` is byte-identical at 68920 and every one of the 1,848 losses is a named `dropped_items` record -- which is the instrument earning its keep: it could distinguish "lossier" from "less truthful", and that distinction is the only reason this is a filing rather than a merge. **T094** is the ninth appearance of the recurring shape, on the far side of a keyword default: a sweep deferred so the fallback could land ahead of it, whose remaining sites could not fail and could not be tested because nothing in the tree could produce the input that breaks them. A deferred sweep with no producer is not latent; it is invisible. Order is now T094, then T091's merge, then T092's routing decision. **T094 CLOSED 2026-08-21 (`da56a24`), and with it T091 -- RC-1 is closed on the pair that motivated it.** The sweep's own count was worth having: five filed sites are seven production calls, because `owned._resolve_target_pos_by_guid` is one signature hiding three callers -- a wrapper conceals its callers exactly the way a keyword default conceals its unswept sites, and grep cannot tell the difference. The trade reversed **to the object**: `MoStemMsa` 164 -> 1951, `MoInflAffMsa` 36 -> 134, `MoDerivAffMsa` 0 -> 3, `MoUnclassifiedAffixMsa` 0 -> 2, `total_shortfall` 72528 -> 70638 (the pre-T091 baseline to the unit), while T091's `PartOfSpeech` row predicate stayed green and its five duplicates stayed gone. The 1,848 losses were not a cascade with its own residue; they were ONE broken lookup applied 1,848 times, and only a census that held `unexplained_shortfall` byte-stable at 68920 through both the breakage AND the repair could show that -- an instrument that merged "lossier" into "worse" would have made T091 look like a bad idea rather than an unfinished one. Two further lessons. First, `_as_pos` now runs INSIDE the natural-key resolver rather than at each call site: this was T088's defect class's third appearance, three of the five swept sites hand the resolver a base-typed object, and putting the cast where the `Name` is read is the first time the fix has been placed where a future site inherits it. Second, `_pos_guid_of` at the 1,848 site had ALREADY read the object it needed and discarded it one line before the call -- two of this feature's ten recurring-shape instances now share that sub-shape, where the value is present, correctly obtained, and dropped just before the place it was required. **T095** joined the out-of-band list from T094's run and is the tenth appearance, the first where what is read at the wrong level is a stated ASSUMPTION rather than a value: `_stash_feature_category_links`'s docstring says "GUIDs are preserved on transfer so target_pos_guid == source pos guid", which T091 ended, so two of the five reused categories lose their `InflectableFeatsRC` wiring -- reported as a Skip, never silent, and costing **0** shortfall because reference-collection membership is not a counted object class. That is the reason it is a filing and not a census row, and the reason T094's own predicate could not have caught it. Order is now T091's merge (branch `038-t091-pos-natural-key` is mergeable), then T092's routing decision, then T095. **T096** joined them 2026-08-21 and arrived by a route none of the others did: **a rebase**. Bringing the branch onto `main` (0 behind, 72 ahead, zero conflicts) turned the integration suite from 1 failure to 2, and the new one was `TestReasonVocabulary::test_tokens_match_the_schema_enum_exactly` -- a tripwire built for exactly this. `census_cli.py` had stamped `DEPENDENCY_UNRESOLVED` on drops whose referent was absent on the SOURCE while its own comment called that "least-wrong ... and still wrong" and left a `TODO(contract)` naming its replacement; `main` had added `SOURCE_REFERENT_ABSENT` to the closed vocabulary on 2026-08-20 (`b2cb356`), and the branch was 48 commits behind and never saw it. **A new variant of the recurring shape**: not a signal read at the wrong level, but code waiting on a contract that had already arrived -- invisible for as long as the branch stayed behind, and surfaced the instant it caught up. The sweep T096 ran for siblings of that shape found three more, filed as **T097** (035's verdict taxonomy left review 2026-08-18; `compare_objects()` is still the two-valued stub), **T098** (035 admitted the six roster classes 2026-08-19; the tripwire meant to fire on that merge has zero consumers) and **T099** (the schema has permitted `null` counts all along; the model cannot express it, so one path emits a knowingly-false `0` and a neighbouring path aborts the run). **T097 remains open; T098 and T099 are closed.** **T098 CLOSED 2026-08-22** (`044d644`), and its lesson is about WHICH half of a mechanism failed: the filing said the designed tripwire did not fire, but the sentence it quoted belongs to `roster_admitted_classes`, which reads 035's roster at run time and fired exactly as promised -- measured live, all seven natural-key classes `roster_admitted: true` with no edit, and `PhNCFeatures` (one of the six) taking a run to DUPLICATE_IDENTITY on 3 extra objects over a difference of 0. What had no reader was `NaturalKeyDefinition.roster_source`, the code's record of why it believes what it believes; the default is gone, the vocabulary is closed and validated, and `verify_roster_sources` now raises before a project is opened. Run over the OLD values the check reports 6 disagreements, over the new ones 0. It also fires in the direction nothing else in the tree can see -- a roster REMOVAL. **T082 was re-checked and narrowed rather than closed**: `038-NK-P2` cannot be settled by a merge at all, and `038-NK-P3` goes from four claims to one class, because the duplicates the natural-key fallback was meant to remove did not go to zero -- they MOVED from `PhPhoneme` to `PhNCFeatures`. **T099 CLOSED 2026-08-21** (`2fce617`) on a back-to-back census pair, and it is the first appearance of the recurring shape where the thing read at the wrong level is the HANDLING itself: `Lib/census.py` already had `Optional[int]` counts, a `difference is None` branch in `row_verdict_class`, a `(0, 0)` early return in `unexplained_counts` and `None not in (...)` guards on invariants 3, 4 and 11 -- about a dozen places of null handling, every one of them DEAD, because `models.ClassCensusRow` declared `source_count: int` and no `ClassCensusRow` could produce the input. The fix added no null handling; it made a feature-long accumulation of it reachable, and the delta is exactly two rows with no gate quantity moved (`total_shortfall` 70659 either side, verdict unchanged). Two things it could not settle joined the out-of-band list: **T100** (the closed 17-token vocabulary has no member meaning "the accessor did not resolve", so a NOT_EVALUATED row cannot always name its reason -- a contract amendment with T096's exact-match tripwire attached, not a code change) and **T101** (the GATE still accepts an uncorroborated null count on a gate-required row and gates exit 0; T099 bounded the PRODUCER instead, because the gate half changes what every artifact already committed is judged by). Both open, and T100 comes first because T101's invariant is written in terms of it. **The lesson is procedural and belongs in the land order**: a long-lived branch does not merely miss new spec, it goes blind to spec written FOR IT -- so rebase before trusting a green suite, not after. **T089 CLOSED 2026-08-22**, and it is the cleanest instance of the recurring shape the feature has produced, because the fix made the signal SMALLER rather than adding a second one beside it. The edge was read at a level where its far endpoint could not do its job; naming the OWNING feature instead of the owned value collapsed 30 unplannable endpoints onto the 4 plannable ones that were already there (8 onto 2 on the second corpus), and the composite `affixes_dependencies` fell 1063 -> 756 and 405 -> 345. Two things it settled that the filing did not predict. First, the shape was in a SHARED helper, so fixing it fixed the phonological twin (`phonemes_dependencies`, whose values `phonological_features_execute_action` co-creates identically) and the POS side at the same time -- the first time in this feature a recurring-shape fix has been global by construction rather than by sweeping. Second, the sweep still found one INDEPENDENT sibling (`variant_types_dependencies`, fixed here, unregistered so no plan moves) and one deliberate non-fix (`natural_classes_dependencies`, whose bare-guid contract makes it a producer-split problem rather than an endpoint problem). The registration the fix unblocks is **T104**, filed rather than folded in, on the task's own instruction. **T104 CLOSED 2026-08-22** (`2358eb5`), and its lesson is about what a filing BUYS. Keeping the two apart cost one extra driver run and bought a measurement neither task could otherwise have made: T089 proved the fix moved no plan with the registry held fixed, T104 proved the registration moves nothing it should not with the producer held fixed, and the two together say something a single combined run could only have asserted. The census also came back row-for-row identical to T076's WITHOUT being asked to compare -- `compare_census_to` is None -- which is the strongest available evidence that a row observable only under a narrow selection really is inert under a full copy.

**The bottleneck is `categories.py`**, at 9,157 lines the primary surface for Phases 4, 5, 6, 7 and 8. Its tasks are serial by necessity, not by oversight, and Phases 5 and 7 must hold the claim for their duration (plan.md:289-290).

**Acceptance rule governing every phase gate above**: a phase is not done when its unit tests pass; it is done when the census run for its predicate exits 0 with the predicate satisfied.
